backend/app/config.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import json 

from typing import Literal 

 

from pydantic import Field 

from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict 

 

 

PIIAction = Literal["redact", "mask", "hash"] 

 

 

class Settings(BaseSettings): 

    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_prefix="", case_sensitive=False) 

 

    # Server 

    APP_NAME: str = "Document Comparison AI Agent (Backend)" 

    LOG_LEVEL: str = "INFO" 

 

    # Security 

    API_KEY: str = Field(default="replace_me_long_random", min_length=12) 

    CORS_ALLOW_ORIGINS: str = Field(default="")  # comma-separated 

    RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE: int = Field(default=60, ge=1, le=6000) 

 

    # LLM 

    OPENAI_API_KEY: str = Field(default="") 

    OPENAI_MODEL: str = Field(default="gpt-4o-mini") 

    OPENAI_EMBEDDING_MODEL: str = Field(default="text-embedding-3-small") 

    OPENAI_TEMPERATURE: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0.0, le=0.0) 

    OPENAI_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS: int = Field(default=800, ge=1, le=4096) 

    OUTBOUND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS: float = Field(default=20.0, ge=1.0, le=120.0) 

 

    # Diff / payload budgets 

    MAX_DIFF_ITEMS: int = Field(default=400, ge=50, le=5000) 

    MAX_TEXT_CHARS_FOR_LLM: int = Field(default=12000, ge=1000, le=100000) 

 

    # Cache 

    CACHE_TTL_SECONDS: int = Field(default=3600, ge=60, le=24 * 3600) 

    SEMANTIC_CACHE_THRESHOLD: float = Field(default=0.92, ge=0.0, le=1.0) 

    MAX_CACHE_ITEMS: int = Field(default=512, ge=64, le=5000) 

 

    # PII masking 

    PII_HASH_SALT: str = Field(default="change_me_salt", min_length=8) 

    PII_CONFIG_JSON: str = Field( 

        default=json.dumps( 

            { 

                "email": "redact", 

                "phone": "mask", 

                "ssn": "redact", 

                "credit_card": "hash", 

                "ip": "mask", 

            } 

        ) 

    ) 

 

    def cors_origins_list(self) -> list[str]: 

        raw = (self.CORS_ALLOW_ORIGINS or "").strip() 

        if not raw: 

            return [] 

        parts = [p.strip() for p in raw.split(",") if p.strip()] 

        return parts 

 

    def pii_config(self) -> dict[str, PIIAction]: 

        try: 

            obj = json.loads(self.PII_CONFIG_JSON or "{}") 

            out: dict[str, PIIAction] = {} 

            for k, v in obj.items(): 

                if v in ("redact", "mask", "hash"): 

                    out[str(k).strip().lower()] = v  # type: ignore[assignment] 

            return out 

        except Exception: 

            return {"email": "redact", "phone": "mask", "ssn": "redact", "credit_card": "hash", "ip": "mask"} 

backend/app/models.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from typing import Any, Literal, Optional 

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, conint, confloat 

 

 

class HealthResponse(BaseModel): 

    ok: bool = True 

    service: str 

    version: str 

 

 

class FileMeta(BaseModel): 

    filename: str 

    content_type: str 

    size_bytes: conint(ge=0) 

    sha256: str 

    detected_type: Literal["pdf", "txt", "csv", "docx", "xlsx", "pptx"] 

 

 

class ChangeItem(BaseModel): 

    kind: Literal["added", "removed", "modified"] 

    ref: str = Field(description="Reference: line range, paragraph index, page number, cell address, etc.") 

    before: Optional[str] = None 

    after: Optional[str] = None 

 

 

class TextDiff(BaseModel): 

    added: conint(ge=0) 

    removed: conint(ge=0) 

    modified: conint(ge=0) 

    items: list[ChangeItem] 

    truncated: bool = False 

 

 

class TabularSchemaDiff(BaseModel): 

    added_columns: list[str] = [] 

    removed_columns: list[str] = [] 

    common_columns: list[str] = [] 

    added_sheets: list[str] = [] 

    removed_sheets: list[str] = [] 

    common_sheets: list[str] = [] 

 

 

class TabularCellChange(BaseModel): 

    sheet: str 

    cell: str 

    before: Optional[str] = None 

    after: Optional[str] = None 

 

 

class TabularDiff(BaseModel): 

    schema: TabularSchemaDiff 

    cell_changes: list[TabularCellChange] = [] 

    truncated: bool = False 

 

 

class DocxDiff(BaseModel): 

    added: conint(ge=0) 

    removed: conint(ge=0) 

    modified: conint(ge=0) 

    items: list[ChangeItem] 

    truncated: bool = False 

 

 

class PdfPageDiff(BaseModel): 

    page: conint(ge=1) 

    diff: TextDiff 

 

 

class PdfDiff(BaseModel): 

    pages: list[PdfPageDiff] 

    truncated: bool = False 

 

 

class DiffPayload(BaseModel): 

    mode: Literal["text", "tabular", "docx", "pdf"] 

    text: Optional[TextDiff] = None 

    tabular: Optional[TabularDiff] = None 

    docx: Optional[DocxDiff] = None 

    pdf: Optional[PdfDiff] = None 

 

 

class LlmSummary(BaseModel): 

    status: Literal["ok", "insufficient_evidence"] 

    overview: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000) 

    key_changes: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=20) 

    risk_notes: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=20) 

    confidence: confloat(ge=0.0, le=1.0) = 0.7 

    cache_hit: bool = False 

 

 

class CompareResponse(BaseModel): 

    request_id: str 

    compare_id: str 

    file_a: FileMeta 

    file_b: FileMeta 

    diff: DiffPayload 

    summary: LlmSummary 

    exact_cache_hit: bool = False 

    semantic_cache_hit: bool = False 

 

 

class ExportSummaryResponse(BaseModel): 

    request_id: str 

    compare_id: str 

    file_a: FileMeta 

    file_b: FileMeta 

    summary: LlmSummary 

 

 

class FeedbackRequest(BaseModel): 

    compare_id: str = Field(min_length=8) 

    rating: conint(ge=1, le=5) 

    comment: str = Field(default="", max_length=2000) 

    metadata: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict) 

 

 

class FeedbackResponse(BaseModel): 

    request_id: str 

    ok: bool = True 

 

 

class ErrorResponse(BaseModel): 

    request_id: str 

    error: str 

    detail: str 

backend/app/hashing.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import hashlib 

from typing import Any 

 

 

def sha256_bytes(data: bytes) -> str: 

    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() 

 

 

def sha256_text(text: str) -> str: 

    return hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8", errors="ignore")).hexdigest() 

 

 

def stable_compact_hash(obj: Any) -> str: 

    # Deterministic hash for cache keys without leaking PII (caller must pass safe object). 

    import json 

 

    payload = json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True) 

    return sha256_text(payload) 

backend/app/pii.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import hashlib 

import re 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

from typing import Callable, Literal 

 

PIIAction = Literal["redact", "mask", "hash"] 

 

 

@dataclass(frozen=True) 

class PIIPattern: 

    name: str 

    regex: re.Pattern 

    masker: Callable[[str], str] 

 

 

_EMAIL_RE = re.compile(r"\b[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}\b", re.IGNORECASE) 

_PHONE_RE = re.compile(r"\b(?:\+?\d{1,3}[-.\s]?)?(?:\(?\d{3}\)?[-.\s]?)?\d{3}[-.\s]?\d{4}\b") 

_SSN_RE = re.compile(r"\b\d{3}-\d{2}-\d{4}\b") 

_CC_RE = re.compile(r"\b(?:\d[ -]*?){13,19}\b") 

_IP_RE = re.compile(r"\b(?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}\b") 

 

 

def _mask_keep_last(s: str, last: int = 4) -> str: 

    s2 = re.sub(r"\s+", "", s) 

    if len(s2) <= last: 

        return "*" * len(s2) 

    return "*" * (len(s2) - last) + s2[-last:] 

 

 

def _mask_email(s: str) -> str: 

    try: 

        user, dom = s.split("@", 1) 

        u = user[:1] + "***" if user else "***" 

        return f"{u}@{dom}" 

    except Exception: 

        return "***@***" 

 

 

def _mask_ip(s: str) -> str: 

    parts = s.split(".") 

    if len(parts) == 4: 

        return ".".join([parts[0], "***", "***", parts[3]]) 

    return "***.***.***.***" 

 

 

def _identity(s: str) -> str: 

    return s 

 

 

PATTERNS: list[PIIPattern] = [ 

    PIIPattern("email", _EMAIL_RE, _mask_email), 

    PIIPattern("phone", _PHONE_RE, lambda s: _mask_keep_last(s, 4)), 

    PIIPattern("ssn", _SSN_RE, lambda s: "***-**-" + _mask_keep_last(s, 4)), 

    PIIPattern("credit_card", _CC_RE, lambda s: _mask_keep_last(s, 4)), 

    PIIPattern("ip", _IP_RE, _mask_ip), 

] 

 

 

def _hash_value(value: str, salt: str) -> str: 

    h = hashlib.sha256((salt + value).encode("utf-8", errors="ignore")).hexdigest() 

    return h 

 

 

def mask_text(text: str, config: dict[str, PIIAction], salt: str) -> str: 

    if not text: 

        return text 

 

    out = text 

 

    for p in PATTERNS: 

        action = config.get(p.name, "redact") 

        if action == "redact": 

            out = p.regex.sub(f"[REDACTED:{p.name.upper()}]", out) 

        elif action == "mask": 

            out = p.regex.sub(lambda m: f"[MASKED:{p.name.upper()}:{p.masker(m.group(0))}]", out) 

        elif action == "hash": 

            out = p.regex.sub(lambda m: f"[HASHED:{p.name.upper()}:{_hash_value(m.group(0), salt)[:12]}]", out) 

        else: 

            out = p.regex.sub(f"[REDACTED:{p.name.upper()}]", out) 

 

    return out 

backend/app/security.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from fastapi import Header, HTTPException, Request 

from starlette.status import HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED 

 

 

def extract_api_key(x_api_key: str | None, authorization: str | None) -> str | None: 

    if x_api_key: 

        return x_api_key.strip() 

    if authorization: 

        auth = authorization.strip() 

        if auth.lower().startswith("bearer "): 

            return auth[7:].strip() 

    return None 

 

 

async def require_api_key( 

    request: Request, 

    x_api_key: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="X-API-Key"), 

    authorization: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="Authorization"), 

) -> str: 

    settings = request.app.state.settings 

    key = extract_api_key(x_api_key, authorization) 

    if not key or key != settings.API_KEY: 

        raise HTTPException(status_code=HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Unauthorized") 

    return key 

backend/app/rate_limit.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import time 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

from typing import Dict, Tuple 

 

from fastapi import HTTPException, Request 

from starlette.status import HTTP_429_TOO_MANY_REQUESTS 

 

 

@dataclass 

class Bucket: 

    window_start: float 

    count: int 

 

 

class RateLimiter: 

    def __init__(self, per_minute: int): 

        self.per_minute = per_minute 

        self._buckets: Dict[str, Bucket] = {} 

 

    def _key(self, request: Request, api_key: str) -> str: 

        ip = request.client.host if request.client else "unknown" 

        return f"{api_key}:{ip}" 

 

    def check(self, request: Request, api_key: str) -> None: 

        now = time.time() 

        key = self._key(request, api_key) 

        b = self._buckets.get(key) 

        if b is None: 

            self._buckets[key] = Bucket(window_start=now, count=1) 

            return 

        if now - b.window_start >= 60.0: 

            b.window_start = now 

            b.count = 1 

            return 

        b.count += 1 

        if b.count > self.per_minute: 

            raise HTTPException(status_code=HTTP_429_TOO_MANY_REQUESTS, detail="Rate limit exceeded") 

 

    def sweep(self) -> None: 

        now = time.time() 

        dead = [k for k, b in self._buckets.items() if (now - b.window_start) >= 120.0] 

        for k in dead: 

            self._buckets.pop(k, None) 

backend/app/audit.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import json 

import os 

import time 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

from typing import Any, Optional 

 

 

@dataclass 

class AuditEvent: 

    ts: float 

    request_id: str 

    path: str 

    method: str 

    status: int 

    latency_ms: int 

    api_key_present: bool 

    file_a_sha256: Optional[str] = None 

    file_b_sha256: Optional[str] = None 

    compare_id: Optional[str] = None 

    exact_cache_hit: Optional[bool] = None 

    semantic_cache_hit: Optional[bool] = None 

    notes: Optional[str] = None 

 

 

class AuditLogger: 

    def __init__(self, log_path: str): 

        self.log_path = log_path 

        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(log_path) or ".", exist_ok=True) 

 

    def write(self, event: AuditEvent) -> None: 

        payload: dict[str, Any] = { 

            "ts": event.ts, 

            "request_id": event.request_id, 

            "path": event.path, 

            "method": event.method, 

            "status": event.status, 

            "latency_ms": event.latency_ms, 

            "api_key_present": event.api_key_present, 

        } 

        for k in ("file_a_sha256", "file_b_sha256", "compare_id", "exact_cache_hit", "semantic_cache_hit", "notes"): 

            v = getattr(event, k) 

            if v is not None: 

                payload[k] = v 

 

        line = json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True) 

        with open(self.log_path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f: 

            f.write(line + "\n") 

 

 

def now_ts() -> float: 

    return time.time() 

backend/app/storage.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import time 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

from typing import Any, Dict, Optional 

 

from .models import CompareResponse, FeedbackRequest 

 

 

@dataclass 

class _Item: 

    value: Any 

    expires_at: float 

 

 

class TTLStore: 

    def __init__(self, ttl_seconds: int, max_items: int = 2048): 

        self.ttl = ttl_seconds 

        self.max_items = max_items 

        self._data: Dict[str, _Item] = {} 

 

    def set(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None: 

        self._evict_if_needed() 

        self._data[key] = _Item(value=value, expires_at=time.time() + self.ttl) 

 

    def get(self, key: str) -> Optional[Any]: 

        it = self._data.get(key) 

        if not it: 

            return None 

        if time.time() > it.expires_at: 

            self._data.pop(key, None) 

            return None 

        return it.value 

 

    def _evict_if_needed(self) -> None: 

        if len(self._data) < self.max_items: 

            return 

        # Remove expired first, then oldest expiry 

        now = time.time() 

        expired = [k for k, v in self._data.items() if now > v.expires_at] 

        for k in expired: 

            self._data.pop(k, None) 

        if len(self._data) < self.max_items: 

            return 

        # Evict by soonest expiry (approx LRU-ish) 

        k_old = min(self._data.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1].expires_at)[0] 

        self._data.pop(k_old, None) 

 

 

class AppStores: 

    def __init__(self, ttl_seconds: int): 

        self.compare_results = TTLStore(ttl_seconds=ttl_seconds, max_items=1024) 

        self.feedback = TTLStore(ttl_seconds=ttl_seconds * 4, max_items=4096) 

 

    def put_compare(self, compare_id: str, resp: CompareResponse) -> None: 

        self.compare_results.set(compare_id, resp) 

 

    def get_compare(self, compare_id: str) -> Optional[CompareResponse]: 

        v = self.compare_results.get(compare_id) 

        return v 

 

    def put_feedback(self, request_id: str, fb: FeedbackRequest) -> None: 

        self.feedback.set(f"{request_id}:{fb.compare_id}:{int(time.time())}", fb.dict()) 

backend/app/utils.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

importfrom fastapi import Requestimport uuid 

 

 

def get_request_id(request: Request) -> str: 

    rid = request.headers.get("X-Request-Id") or request.headers.get("X-Request-ID") 

    if rid and 8 <= len(rid) <= 128: 

        return rid 

    return str(uuid.uuid4()) 

 

 

def sse_event(event: str, data: str) -> str: 

    safe_event = (event or "message").replace("\n", " ").strip() 

    # SSE requires lines starting with "data:" 

    lines = (data or "").splitlines() or [""] 

    payload = f"event: {safe_event}\n" + "\n".join([f"data: {ln}" for ln in lines]) + "\n\n" 

    return payload

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from dataclasses import_TYPEfrom dataclasses import dataclass 

 

from ..hashing import sha256_bytes 

from .text import parse_text 

from .pdf import parse_pdf 

from .docx import parse_docx 

from .pptx import parse_pptx 

from .tabular import parse_csv, parse_xlsx 

 

 

DocType = Literal["pdf", "txt", "csv", "docx", "xlsx", "pptx"] 

 

 

@dataclass 

class DetectedDoc: 

    doc_type: DocType 

    sha256: str 

    filename: str 

    content_type: str 

    size_bytes: int 

    parsed: dict  # type-specific structure 

 

 

def _detect_type(filename: str) -> Optional[DocType]: 

    f = (filename or "").lower().strip() 

    if f.endswith(".pdf"): 

        return "pdf" 

    if f.endswith(".txt"): 

        return "txt" 

    if f.endswith(".csv"): 

        return "csv" 

    if f.endswith(".docx"): 

        return "docx" 

    if f.endswith(".xlsx"): 

        return "xlsx" 

    if f.endswith(".pptx"): 

        return "pptx" 

    return None 

 

 

async def parse_upload(file: UploadFile) -> DetectedDoc: 

    filename = file.filename or "upload" 

    doc_type = _detect_type(filename) 

    if not doc_type: 

        raise HTTPException( 

            status_code=HTTP_415_UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE, 

            detail="Unsupported file type. Allowed: pdf, txt, csv, docx, xlsx, pptx", 

        ) 

 

    raw = await file.read() 

    h = sha256_bytes(raw) 

    size = len(raw) 

    ctype = file.content_type or "application/octet-stream" 

 

    bio = BytesIO(raw) 

    if doc_type == "txt": 

        parsed = parse_text(bio) 

    elif doc_type == "pdf": 

        parsed = parse_pdf(bio) 

    elif doc_type == "docx": 

        parsed = parse_docx(bio) 

    elif doc_type == "pptx": 

        parsed = parse_pptx(bio) 

    elif doc_type == "csv": 

        parsed = parse_csv(bio) 

    elif doc_type == "xlsx": 

        parsed = parse_xlsx(bio) 

    else: 

        raise HTTPException( 

            status_code=HTTP_415_UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE, 

            detail="Unsupported file type", 

        ) 

 

    return DetectedDoc( 

        doc_type=doc_type, 

        sha256=h, 

        filename=filename, 

        content_type=ctype, 

        size_bytes=size, 

        parsed=parsed, 

    ) 

from io import BytesIO 

from typing import Literal, Optional 

 

from fastapi import HTTPException, UploadFile

    except Exception:

    return

Backend/app/parsing/docx.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import difflib 

 

from ..models import ChangeItem, DocxDiff 

 

 

def _clip(s: str, max_chars: int = 800) -> str: 

    s = s or "" 

    if len(s) <= max_chars: 

        return s 

    return s[: max_chars - 12] + "…(truncated)" 

 

 

def diff_docx(a_paras: list[str], b_paras: list[str], max_items: int) -> DocxDiff: 

    sm = difflib.SequenceMatcher(a=a_paras, b=b_paras) 

    items: list[ChangeItem] = [] 

    added = removed = modified = 0 

    truncated = False 

 

    for tag, i1, i2, j1, j2 in sm.get_opcodes(): 

        if tag == "equal": 

            continue 

        ref = f"paragraphs a[{i1}:{i2}) ↔ b[{j1}:{j2})" 

        if tag == "insert": 

            added += (j2 - j1) 

            after = "\n".join(b_paras[j1:j2]) 

            items.append(ChangeItem(kind="added", ref=ref, after=_clip(after))) 

        elif tag == "delete": 

            removed += (i2 - i1) 

            before = "\n".join(a_paras[i1:i2]) 

            items.append(ChangeItem(kind="removed", ref=ref, before=_clip(before))) 

        elif tag == "replace": 

            modified += max(i2 - i1, j2 - j1) 

            before = "\n".join(a_paras[i1:i2]) 

            after = "\n".join(b_paras[j1:j2]) 

            items.append(ChangeItem(kind="modified", ref=ref, before=_clip(before), after=_clip(after))) 

 

        if len(items) >= max_items: 

            truncated = True 

            break 

 

    return DocxDiff(added=added, removed=removed, modified=modified, items=items, truncated=truncated) 

 

 

 

backend/app/parsing/pptx.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from io import BytesIO 

from typing import List, Dict 

 

from pptx import Presentation 

 

 

def parse_pptx(bio: BytesIO) -> dict: 

    bio.seek(0) 

    slides: List[Dict[str, str]] = [] 

    try: 

        prs = Presentation(bio) 

        for i, slide in enumerate:        for i, slide in enumerate(prs.slides): 

        slides = [] 

    full_text = "\n\n".join([f"[SLIDE {s['slide']}]\n{s['text']}".strip() for s in slides]).strip() 

    return {"slides": slides, "text": full_text} 

            texts: list[str] = [] 

            for shape in slide.shapes: 

                if hasattr(shape, "text") and shape.text: 

                    texts.append(shape.text) 

            slide_text = "\n".join(texts).strip() 

            slides.append({"slide": str(i + 1), "text": slide_text}) 

backend/app/parsing/tabular.py 

from __future__ import annotationsfrom __from io import BytesIO, TextIOWrapper 

from typing import Any, Dict, List 

 

from openpyxl import load_workbook 

 

 

def parse_csv(bio: BytesIO) -> dict: 

    bio.seek(0) 

    wrapper = TextIOWrapper(bio, encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore", newline="") 

    sample = wrapper.read(4096) 

    wrapper.seek(0) 

    try: 

        dialect = csv.Sniffer().sniff(sample) 

    except Exception: 

        dialect = csv.excel 

    reader = csv.reader(wrapper, dialect) 

    rows: list[list[str]] = [] 

    for r in reader: 

        rows.append([c if c is not None else "" for c in r]) 

    header = rows[0] if rows else [] 

    data_rows = rows[1:] if len(rows) > 1 else [] 

    return {"type": "csv", "sheets": {"__csv__": {"header": header, "rows": data_rows}}} 

 

 

def _cell_to_str(v: Any) -> str: 

    if v is None: 

        return "" 

    if isinstance(v, str): 

        return v 

    return str(v) 

 

 

def parse_xlsx(bio: BytesIO) -> dict: 

    bio.seek(0) 

    sheets: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {} 

    try: 

        wb = load_workbook(filename=bio, read_only=True, data_only=True) 

        for name in wb.sheetnames: 

            ws = wb[name] 

            values: list[list[str]] = [] 

            for row in ws.iter_rows(values_only=True): 

                values.append([_cell_to_str(v) for v in row]) 

            header = values[0] if values else [] 

            data_rows = values[1:] if len(values) > 1 else [] 

            sheets[name] = {"header": header, "rows": data_rows} 

    except Exception: 

        sheets = {} 

    return {"type": "xlsx", "sheets": sheets}

backend/app/diff/text_diff.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import difflib 

from typing import List, Tuple 

 

from ..models import ChangeItem, TextDiff 

 

 

def _clip(s: str, max_chars: int = 800) -> str: 

    s = s or "" 

    if len(s) <= max_chars: 

        return s 

    return s[: max_chars - 12] + "…(truncated)" 

 

 

def diff_text(a: str, b: str, max_items: int) -> TextDiff: 

    a_lines = (a or "").splitlines() 

    b_lines = (b or "").splitlines() 

 

    sm = difflib.SequenceMatcher(a=a_lines, b=b_lines) 

    items: list[ChangeItem] = [] 

    added = removed = modified = 0 

    truncated = False 

 

    for tag, i1, i2, j1, j2 in sm.get_opcodes(): 

        if tag == "equal": 

            continue 

        ref = f"lines a[{i1+1}:{i2}] ↔ b[{j1+1}:{j2}]" 

        if tag == "insert": 

            added += (j2 - j1) 

            after = "\n".join(b_lines[j1:j2]) 

            items.append(ChangeItem(kind="added", ref=ref, before=None, after=_clip(after))) 

        elif tag == "delete": 

            removed += (i2 - i1) 

            before = "\n".join(a_lines[i1:i2]) 

            items.append(ChangeItem(kind="removed", ref=ref, before=_clip(before), after=None)) 

        elif tag == "replace": 

            modified += max(i2 - i1, j2 - j1) 

            before = "\n".join(a_lines[i1:i2]) 

            after = "\n".join(b_lines[j1:j2]) 

            items.append(ChangeItem(kind="modified", ref=ref, before=_clip(before), after=_clip(after))) 

 

        if len(items) >= max_items: 

            truncated = True 

            break 

 

    return TextDiff(added=added, removed=removed, modified=modified, items=items, truncated=truncated) 

backend/app/diff/docx_diff.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import difflib 

 

from ..models import ChangeItem, DocxDiff 

 

 

def _clip(s: str, max_chars: int = 800) -> str: 

    s = s or "" 

    if len(s) <= max_chars: 

        return s 

    return s[: max_chars - 12] + "…(truncated)" 

 

 

def diff_docx(a_paras: list[str], b_paras: list[str], max_items: int) -> DocxDiff: 

    sm = difflib.SequenceMatcher(a=a_paras, b=b_paras) 

    items: list[ChangeItem] = [] 

    added = removed = modified = 0 

    truncated = False 

 

    for tag, i1, i2, j1, j2 in sm.get_opcodes(): 

        if tag == "equal": 

            continue 

        ref = f"paragraphs a[{i1}:{i2}) ↔ b[{j1}:{j2})" 

        if tag == "insert": 

            added += (j2 - j1) 

            after = "\n".join(b_paras[j1:j2]) 

            items.append(ChangeItem(kind="added", ref=ref, after=_clip(after))) 

        elif tag == "delete": 

            removed += (i2 - i1) 

            before = "\n".join(a_paras[i1:i2]) 

            items.append(ChangeItem(kind="removed", ref=ref, before=_clip(before))) 

        elif tag == "replace": 

            modified += max(i2 - i1, j2 - j1) 

            before = "\n".join(a_paras[i1:i2]) 

            after = "\n".join(b_paras[j1:j2]) 

            items.append(ChangeItem(kind="modified", ref=ref, before=_clip(before), after=_clip(after))) 

 

        if len(items) >= max_items: 

            truncated = True 

            break 

 

    return DocxDiff(added=added, removed=removed, modified=modified, items=items, truncated=truncated) 

backend/app/diff/pdf_diff.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from ..models import PdfDiff, PdfPageDiff 

from .text_diff import diff_text 

 

 

def diff_pdf(a_pages: list[dict], b_pages: list[dict], max_items: int) -> PdfDiff: 

    # Best-effort: align by page number, compare text per page, include page refs. 

    a_map = {int(p.get("page", "0") or 0): (p.get("text") or "") for p in (a_pages or []) if (p.get("page") or "").isdigit()} 

    b_map = {int(p.get("page", "0") or 0): (p.get("text") or "") for p in (b_pages or []) if (p.get("page") or "").isdigit()} 

    pages = sorted(set(a_map.keys()) | set(b_map.keys())) 

    out: list[PdfPageDiff] = [] 

    truncated = False 

 

    # Distribute max_items budget across pages (cap per page) 

    per_page_budget = max(20, max_items // max(1, min(len(pages), 10))) 

 

    for pg in pages: 

        dt = diff_text(a_map.get(pg, ""), b_map.get(pg, ""), max_items=per_page_budget) 

        out.append(PdfPageDiff(page=pg, diff=dt)) 

        if len(out) >= 25: 

            truncated = True 

            break 

 

    return PdfDiff(pages=out, truncated=truncated) 

backend/app/diff/tabular_diff.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from typing import Dict, List, Tuple 

 

from ..models import TabularCellChange, TabularDiff, TabularSchemaDiff 

 

 

def()def _safe_str(x) -> str: 

    common_cols_set: set[str] = set() 

 

    for sh in common: 

        ah = _headers(a_sheets[sh]) 

        bh = _headers(b_sheets[sh]) 

        aset, bset = set(ah), set(bh) 

        added_cols_set |= (bset - aset) 

        removed_cols_set |= (aset - bset) 

        common_cols_set |= (aset & bset) 

 

    schema.added_columns = sorted(added_cols_set) 

    schema.removed_columns = sorted(removed_cols_set) 

    schema.common_columns = sorted(common_cols_set) 

 

    # Cell diffs: compare by coordinates within common sheets using rectangular intersection. 

    for sh in common: 

        a_sheet = a_sheets[sh] 

        b_sheet = b_sheets[sh] 

        a_all = [_headers(a_sheet)] + _rows(a_sheet) 

        b_all = [_headers(b_sheet)] + _rows(b_sheet) 

 

        rmax = min(len(a_all), len(b_all)) 

        cmax = min(max((len(r) for r in a_all), default=0), max((len(r) for r in b_all), default=0)) 

 

        # compare intersection 

        for r in range(rmax): 

            ar = a_all[r] 

            br = b_all[r] 

            for c in range(cmax): 

                av = ar[c] if c < len(ar) else "" 

                bv = br[c] if c < len(br) else "" 

                if av != bv: 

                    cell = f"R{r+1}C{c+1}" 

                    changes.append(TabularCellChange(sheet=sh, cell=cell, before=av, after=bv)) 

                    if len(changes) >= max_items: 

                        truncated = True 

                        break 

            if truncated: 

                break 

        if truncated: 

            break 

 

    return TabularDiff(schema=schema, cell_changes=changes, truncated=truncated) 

    if x is None: 

        return "" 

    return str(x) 

 

 

def _headers(sheet: dict) -> list[str]: 

    return [str(c) for c in (sheet.get("header") or [])] 

 

 

def _rows(sheet: dict) -> list[list[str]]: 

    return [[_safe_str(c) for c in r] for r in (sheet.get("rows") or [])] 

 

 

def diff_tabular(a: dict, b: dict, max_items: int) -> TabularDiff: 

    a_sheets: Dict[str, dict] = (a or {}).get("sheets") or {} 

    b_sheets: Dict[str, dict] = (b or {}).get("sheets") or {} 

 

    a_names = set(a_sheets.keys()) 

    b_names = set(b_sheets.keys()) 

    common = sorted(a_names & b_names) 

    added_s = sorted(b_names - a_names) 

    removed_s = sorted(a_names - b_names) 

 

    schema = TabularSchemaDiff( 

        added_sheets=added_s, 

        removed_sheets=removed_s, 

        common_sheets=common, 

    ) 

 

    changes: list[TabularCellChange] = [] 

    truncated = False 

 

    # Schema diff for columns (per common sheets): aggregate unique 

    added_cols_set: set[str] = set()

backend/app/llm/cache.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import time 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

from typing import Any, Optional 

 

 

@dataclass 

class _Entry: 

    value: Any 

    expires_at: float 

 

 

class TTLCache: 

    def __init__(self, ttl_seconds: int, max_items: int): 

        self.ttl = ttl_seconds 

        self.max_items = max_items 

        self._d: dict[str, _Entry] = {} 

 

    def get(self, key: str) -> Optional[Any]: 

        e = self._d.get(key) 

        if not e: 

            return None 

        if time.time() > e.expires_at: 

            self._d.pop(key, None) 

            return None 

        return e.value 

 

    def set(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None: 

        self._evict_if_needed() 

        self._d[key] = _Entry(value=value, expires_at=time.time() + self.ttl) 

 

    def _evict_if_needed(self) -> None: 

        if len(self._d) < self.max_items: 

            return 

        now = time.time() 

        expired = [k for k, v in self._d.items() if now > v.expires_at] 

        for k in expired: 

            self._d.pop(k, None) 

        if len(self._d) < self.max_items: 

            return 

        k_old = min(self._d.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1].expires_at)[0] 

        self._d.pop(k_old, None) 

 

    def items(self): 

        now = time.time() 

        for k, v in list(self._d.items()): 

            if now > v.expires_at: 

                self._d.pop(k, None) 

        return self._d.items() 

 

 

class ExactCache(TTLCache): 

    pass 

 

 

class EmbeddingCache(TTLCache): 

    pass 

 

 

class SemanticCache: 

    def __init__(self, ttl_seconds: int, max_items: int, threshold: float): 

        self.store = TTLCache(ttl_seconds=ttl_seconds, max_items=max_items) 

        self.threshold = threshold 

 

    def _cosine(self, a: list[float], b: list[float]) -> float: 

        if not a or not b or len(a) != len(b): 

            return 0.0 

        dot = 0.0 

        na = 0.0 

        nb = 0.0 

        for x, y in zip(a, b): 

            dot += x * y 

            na += x * x 

            nb += y * y 

        if na <= 0.0 or nb <= 0.0: 

            return 0.0 

        import math 

        return dot / (math.sqrt(na) * math.sqrt(nb)) 

 

    def lookup(self, embedding: list[float]) -> tuple[bool, Any]: 

        best = 0.0 

        best_val = None 

        for _, entry in self.store.items(): 

            e = entry.value 

            emb = e.get("embedding") 

            if isinstance(emb, list): 

                score = self._cosine(embedding, emb) 

                if score >= self.threshold and score > best: 

                    best = score 

                    best_val = e.get("value") 

        return (best_val is not None), best_val 

 

    def insert(self, embedding: list[float], value: Any, key_hint: str) -> None: 

        # key_hint used only to manage TTL store keys 

        self.store.set(key_hint, {"embedding": embedding, "value": value})

from __future__ import annotations

def embedding_cache_key(model: str, text: str) -> str: 

    # Text is expected to be PII-masked already by caller.

backend/app/llm/client.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import json 

from typing import Any, Optional 

 

import httpx 

from openai import OpenAI 

 

from ..hashing import stable_compact_hash 

from ..models import LlmSummary 

 

 

SUMMARY_JSON_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = { 

    "name": "DocCompareSummary", 

    "schema": { 

        "type": "object", 

        "additionalProperties": False, 

        "properties": { 

            "status": {"type": "string", "enum": ["ok", "insufficient_evidence"]}, 

            "overview": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 2000}, 

            "key_changes": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "maxItems": 20}, 

            "risk_notes": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "maxItems": 20}, 

            "confidence": {"type": "number", "minimum": 0.0, "maximum": 1.0}, 

        }, 

        "required": ["status", "overview", "key_changes", "risk_notes", "confidence"], 

    }, 

} 

 

 

class LLMClient: 

    def __init__(self, api_key: str, model: str, embed_model: str, max_tokens: int, timeout_s: float): 

        self.api_key = api_key 

        self.model = model 

        self.embed_model = embed_model 

        self.max_tokens = max_tokens 

        self.timeout_s = timeout_s 

 

        # HTTPS-only outbound: enforce base_url scheme and httpx client configuration. 

        base_url = "https://api.openai.com/v1" 

        if not base_url.startswith("https://"): 

            raise RuntimeError("Outbound calls must be HTTPS-only") 

 

        self._http = httpx.Client( 

            timeout=httpx.Timeout(timeout_s), 

            verify=True, 

            follow_redirects=False, 

            trust_env=False, 

        ) 

        self._client = OpenAI(api_key=api_key, base_url=base_url, http_client=self._http) 

 

    def close(self) -> None: 

        try: 

            self._http.close() 

        except Exception: 

            pass 

 

    def embed(self, text: str) -> list[float]: 

        # Deterministic embedding for masked text. 

        resp = self._client.embeddings.create(model=self.embed_model, input=text) 

        return list(resp.data[0].embedding) 

 

    def summarize(self, prompt: str) -> LlmSummary: 

        # Deterministic generation enforced by temperature fixed at 0.0 and max_tokens. 

        # Attempt JSON Schema structured output via Responses API; fallback to JSON object parsing. 

        try: 

            r = self._client.responses.create( 

                model=self.model, 

                input=prompt, 

                temperature=0.0, 

                max_output_tokens=self.max_tokens, 

                response_format={"type": "json_schema", "json_schema": SUMMARY_JSON_SCHEMA}, 

            ) 

            text = getattr(r, "output_text", None) or "" 

            obj = json.loads(text) 

            return LlmSummary(**obj) 

        except Exception: 

            # Fallback: request JSON object and validate 

            r2 = self._client.chat.completions.create( 

                model=self.model, 

                messages=[ 

                    {"role": "system", "content": "Return ONLY valid JSON matching the required fields."}, 

                    {"role": "user", "content": prompt}, 

                ], 

                temperature=0.0, 

                max_tokens=self.max_tokens, 

                response_format={"type": "json_object"}, 

            ) 

            content = (r2.choices[0].message.content or "").strip() 

            obj2 = json.loads(content) 

            return LlmSummary(**obj2) 

 

    @staticmethod 

    def exact_key(parts: dict[str, Any]) -> str: 

        return "exact:" + stable_compact_hash(parts) 

 

    @staticmethod 

    def semantic_key_hint(parts: dict[str, Any]) -> str: 

        return "sem:" + stable_compact_hash(parts)

@app.middleware("http") 

async def audit_and_request_id(request: Request, call_next): 

    rid = get_request_id(request) 

    request.state.request_id = rid 

 

    start = time.time() 

    status = 500 

    api_key_present = bool(request.headers.get("X-API-Key") or request.headers.get("Authorization")) 

    compare_id = None 

    file_a_sha = None 

    file_b_sha = None 

    exact_hit = None 

    semantic_hit = None 

 

    try: 

        response = await call_next(request) 

        status = response.status_code 

        response.headers["X-Request-Id"] = rid 

        return response 

    finally: 

        latency_ms = int((time.time() - start) * 1000) 

        notes = None 

        # Endpoint handlers may set these on request.state (never raw content). 

        compare_id = getattr(request.state, "compare_id", None) 

        file_a_sha = getattr(request.state, "file_a_sha256", None) 

        file_b_sha = getattr(request.state, "file_b_sha256", None) 

        exact_hit = getattr(request.state, "exact_cache_hit", None) 

        semantic_hit = getattr(request.state, "semantic_cache_hit", None) 

        if getattr(request.state, "rate_limited", False): 

            notes = "rate_limited" 

        app.state.audit.write( 

            AuditEvent( 

                ts=now_ts(), 

                request_id=rid, 

                path=str(request.url.path), 

                method=request.method, 

                status=status, 

                latency_ms=latency_ms, 

                api_key_present=api_key_present, 

                file_a_sha256=file_a_sha, 

                file_b_sha256=file_b_sha, 

                compare_id=compare_id, 

                exact_cache_hit=exact_hit, 

                semantic_cache_hit=semantic_hit, 

                notes=notes, 

            ) 

        ) 

 

 

@app.exception_handler(HTTPException) 

async def http_exc_handler(request: Request, exc: HTTPException): 

    rid = getattr(request.state, "request_id", str(uuid.uuid4())) 

    payload = ErrorResponse(request_id=rid, error="http_error", detail=str(exc.detail)) 

    return JSONResponse(status_code=exc.status_code, content=payload.model_dump()) 

 

 

@app.get("/health", response_model=HealthResponse) 

async def health(): 

    return HealthResponse(ok=True, service=settings.APP_NAME, version=__version__) 

 

 

def _file_meta(d) -> FileMeta: 

    return FileMeta( 

        filename=d.filename, 

        content_type=d.content_type, 

        size_bytes=d.size_bytes, 

        sha256=d.sha256, 

        detected_type=d.doc_type, 

    ) 

 

 

def _build_llm_prompt(diff_payload: DiffPayload, file_a: FileMeta, file_b: FileMeta, max_chars: int) -> str: 

    # Keep token-safe prompt: include mode + small snippet of changes only. 

    base = { 

        "mode": diff_payload.mode, 

        "file_a": {"name": file_a.filename, "type": file_a.detected_type, "sha256": file_a.sha256}, 

        "file_b": {"name": file_b.filename, "type": file_b.detected_type, "sha256": file_b.sha256}, 

    } 

 

    diff_part: dict[str, Any] = {"summary": {}} 

 

    if diff_payload.mode == "text" and diff_payload.text: 

        d = diff_payload.text 

        diff_part["summary"] = {"added": d.added, "removed": d.removed, "modified": d.modified, "truncated": d.truncated} 

        diff_part["items"] = [i.model_dump() for i in d.items[:80]] 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "docx" and diff_payload.docx: 

        d = diff_payload.docx 

        diff_part["summary"] = {"added": d.added, "removed": d.removed, "modified": d.modified, "truncated": d.truncated} 

        diff_part["items"] = [i.model_dump() for i in d.items[:80]] 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "tabular" and diff_payload.tabular: 

        d = diff_payload.tabular 

        diff_part["schema"] = d.schema.model_dump() 

        diff_part["cell_changes"] = [c.model_dump() for c in d.cell_changes[:120]] 

        diff_part["truncated"] = d.truncated 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "pdf" and diff_payload.pdf: 

        d = diff_payload.pdf 

        diff_part["pages"] = [{"page": p.page, "diff": {"added": p.diff.added, "removed": p.diff.removed, "modified": p.diff.modified}} for p in d.pages[:12]] 

        # add a few snippets for first pages 

        snippets = [] 

        for p in d.pages[:6]: 

            snippets.extend([i.model_dump() for i in p.diff.items[:10]]) 

        diff_part["items"] = snippets 

        diff_part["truncated"] = d.truncated 

 

    prompt_obj = { 

        "task": "Summarize differences between two documents. Do not guess missing info. Keep concise and business-friendly.", 

        "requirements": { 

            "output_fields": ["status", "overview", "key_changes", "risk_notes", "confidence"], 

            "status_rule": "Use insufficient_evidence if diff content is empty or too truncated to be confident.", 

        }, 

        "context": base, 

        "diff": diff_part, 

    } 

 

    s = json.dumps(prompt_obj, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True) 

    if len(s) > max_chars: 

        s = s[: max_chars - 20] + '"...TRUNCATED"}' 

    user_prompt = ( 

        "You are a deterministic compliance-grade summarizer.\n" 

        "Return ONLY JSON matching the schema fields:\n" 

        '{"status":"ok|insufficient_evidence","overview":"...","key_changes":["..."],"risk_notes":["..."],"confidence":0.0}\n' 

        "Input:\n" 

        f"{s}" 

    ) 

    return user_prompt 

 

 

def _make_llm(settings: Settings) -> LLMClient: 

    if not settings.OPENAI_API_KEY: 

        # No API key: return an "insufficient_evidence" summary deterministically without calling outbound. 

        raise RuntimeError("OPENAI_API_KEY not set") 

    return LLMClient( 

        api_key=settings.OPENAI_API_KEY, 

        model=settings.OPENAI_MODEL, 

        embed_model=settings.OPENAI_EMBEDDING_MODEL, 

        max_tokens=settings.OPENAI_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS, 

        timeout_s=settings.OUTBOUND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS, 

    ) 

 

 

def _masked(settings: Settings, text: str) -> str: 

    return mask_text(text, config=settings.pii_config(), salt=settings.PII_HASH_SALT) 

 

 

def _compute_diff(settings: Settings, a_doc, b_doc) -> DiffPayload: 

    max_items = settings.MAX_DIFF_ITEMS 

 

    # Prefer specialized diffs when both are same family; else fallback to text diff of extracted text. 

    if a_doc.doc_type == "csv" and b_doc.doc_type == "csv": 

        td: TabularDiff = diff_tabular(a_doc.parsed, b_doc.parsed, max_items=max_items) 

        return DiffPayload(mode="tabular", tabular=td) 

 

    if a_doc.doc_type == "xlsx" and b_doc.doc_type == "xlsx": 

        td2: TabularDiff = diff_tabular(a_doc.parsed, b_doc.parsed, max_items=max_items) 

        return DiffPayload(mode="tabular", tabular=td2) 

 

    if a_doc.doc_type == "docx" and b_doc.doc_type == "docx": 

        a_paras = [p.get("text") or "" for p in (a_doc.parsed.get("paragraphs") or [])] 

        b_paras = [p.get("text") or "" for p in (b_doc.parsed.get("paragraphs") or [])] 

        dd: DocxDiff = diff_docx(a_paras, b_paras, max_items=max_items) 

        return DiffPayload(mode="docx", docx=dd) 

 

    if a_doc.doc_type == "pdf" and b_doc.doc_type == "pdf": 

        pd: PdfDiff = diff_pdf(a_doc.parsed.get("pages") or [], b_doc.parsed.get("pages") or [], max_items=max_items) 

        return DiffPayload(mode="pdf", pdf=pd) 

 

    # Text mode for txt/pptx/mixed types: compare extracted text 

    a_text = a_doc.parsed.get("text") or "" 

    b_text = b_doc.parsed.get("text") or "" 

    td3: TextDiff = diff_text(a_text, b_text, max_items=max_items) 

    return DiffPayload(mode="text", text=td3) 

 

 

def _offline_summary(diff_payload: DiffPayload) -> LlmSummary: 

    # Deterministic fallback when OPENAI_API_KEY not configured. 

    empty = False 

    if diff_payload.mode == "text": 

        empty = not diff_payload.text or (diff_payload.text.added + diff_payload.text.removed + diff_payload.text.modified == 0) 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "docx": 

        empty = not diff_payload.docx or (diff_payload.docx.added + diff_payload.docx.removed + diff_payload.docx.modified == 0) 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "tabular": 

        empty = not diff_payload.tabular or (len(diff_payload.tabular.cell_changes) == 0 and not diff_payload.tabular.schema.added_columns and not diff_payload.tabular.schema.removed_columns) 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "pdf": 

        empty = not diff_payload.pdf or (len(diff_payload.pdf.pages) == 0) 

 

    status = "insufficient_evidence" if empty else "ok" 

    overview = "OpenAI not configured; returning deterministic summary from diff statistics." 

    if status == "ok": 

        overview = "Differences detected; OpenAI not configured so detailed narrative summary is unavailable." 

    return LlmSummary(status=status, overview=overview, key_changes=[], risk_notes=[], confidence=0.3, cache_hit=False) 

 

 

async def _rate_limit(request: Request, api_key: str): 

    try: 

        request.app.state.rate_limiter.check(request, api_key) 

    except HTTPException: 

        request.state.rate_limited = True 

        raise 

 

 

@app.post("/compare", response_model=CompareResponse) 

async def compare( 

    request: Request, 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

    file_a: UploadFile = File(...), 

    file_b: UploadFile = File(...), 

): 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

    rid = request.state.request_id 

 

    a_doc = await parse_upload(file_a) 

    b_doc = await parse_upload(file_b) 

 

    # Mask content before any storage / LLM / response. 

    a_doc.parsed = _mask_parsed(settings, a_doc.parsed) 

    b_doc.parsed = _mask_parsed(settings, b_doc.parsed) 

 

    request.state.file_a_sha256 = a_doc.sha256 

    request.state.file_b_sha256 = b_doc.sha256 

 

    # Exact cache key includes attachment hashes and security-relevant options. 

    key_parts = { 

        "a": a_doc.sha256, 

        "b": b_doc.sha256, 

        "types": [a_doc.doc_type, b_doc.doc_type], 

        "max_items": settings.MAX_DIFF_ITEMS, 

        "pii_cfg": settings.pii_config(), 

        "model": settings.OPENAI_MODEL, 

        "embed_model": settings.OPENAI_EMBEDDING_MODEL, 

        "max_tokens": settings.OPENAI_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS, 

        "threshold": settings.SEMANTIC_CACHE_THRESHOLD, 

    } 

    exact_key = request.app.state.llm_key = stable_compact_hash(key_parts) 

 

    cached = request.app.state.exact_cache.get(exact_key) 

    if cached: 

        resp: CompareResponse = cached 

        resp2 = resp.model_copy(deep=True) 

        resp2.request_id = rid 

        resp2.exact_cache_hit = True 

        request.state.exact_cache_hit = True 

        request.state.semantic_cache_hit = getattr(resp, "semantic_cache_hit", False) 

        request.state.compare_id = resp2.compare_id 

        return resp2 

 

    diff_payload = _compute_diff(settings, a_doc, b_doc) 

 

    file_meta_a = _file_meta(a_doc) 

    file_meta_b = _file_meta(b_doc) 

 

    # Semantic cache uses embedding of prompt (masked) and returns prior summary+diff if similar enough. 

    prompt = _build_llm_prompt(diff_payload, file_meta_a, file_meta_b, settings.MAX_TEXT_CHARS_FOR_LLM) 

 

    semantic_hit = False 

    semantic_val = None 

    embedding = None 

    llm_summary: LlmSummary 

 

    try: 

        llm = _make_llm(settings) 

        try: 

            emb_key = embedding_cache_key(settings.OPENAI_EMBEDDING_MODEL, prompt) 

            embedding = request.app.state.embedding_cache.get(emb_key) 

            if embedding is None: 

                embedding = llm.embed(prompt) 

                request.app.state.embedding_cache.set(emb_key, embedding) 

 

            semantic_hit, semantic_val = request.app.state.semantic_cache.lookup(embedding) 

            if semantic_hit and semantic_val: 

                resp_prev: CompareResponse = semantic_val 

                resp_prev2 = resp_prev.model_copy(deep=True) 

                resp_prev2.request_id = rid 

                resp_prev2.semantic_cache_hit = True 

                resp_prev2.summary.cache_hit = True 

                request.state.semantic_cache_hit = True 

                request.state.compare_id = resp_prev2.compare_id 

                request.app.state.exact_cache.set(exact_key, resp_prev2.model_copy(deep=True)) 

                request.state.exact_cache_hit = False 

                return resp_prev2 

 

            llm_summary = llm.summarize(prompt) 

        finally: 

            llm.close() 

    except Exception: 

        llm_summary = _offline_summary(diff_payload) 

 

    compare_id = str(uuid.uuid4()) 

    request.state.compare_id = compare_id 

 

    resp = CompareResponse( 

        request_id=rid, 

        compare_id=compare_id, 

        file_a=file_meta_a, 

        file_b=file_meta_b, 

        diff=diff_payload, 

        summary=llm_summary, 

        exact_cache_hit=False, 

        semantic_cache_hit=bool(semantic_hit), 

    ) 

 

    # Store compare result (masked) + caches 

    request.app.state.stores.put_compare(compare_id, resp) 

    request.app.state.exact_cache.set(exact_key, resp) 

 

    if embedding and not semantic_hit: 

        hint = stable_compact_hash({"a": a_doc.sha256, "b": b_doc.sha256, "mode": diff_payload.mode, "t": time.time()}) 

        request.app.state.semantic_cache.insert(embedding=embedding, value=resp, key_hint=hint) 

 

    request.state.exact_cache_hit = False 

    request.state.semantic_cache_hit = bool(semantic_hit) 

    return resp 

 

 

def _mask_parsed(settings: Settings, parsed: dict) -> dict: 

    # Ensure PII masking before storage, LLM, UI, exports, logs. 

    if not parsed: 

        return parsed 

    cfg = settings.pii_config() 

    salt = settings.PII_HASH_SALT 

 

    def m(s: str) -> str: 

        return mask_text(s or "", cfg, salt) 

 

    out = dict(parsed) 

 

    if "text" in out and isinstance(out["text"], str): 

        out["text"] = m(out["text"]) 

 

    if "pages" in out and isinstance(out["pages"], list): 

        new_pages = [] 

        for p in out["pages"]: 

            if isinstance(p, dict): 

                p2 = dict(p) 

                if isinstance(p2.get("text"), str): 

                    p2["text"] = m(p2["text"]) 

                new_pages.append(p2) 

        out["pages"] = new_pages 

 

    if "paragraphs" in out and isinstance(out["paragraphs"], list): 

        new_paras = [] 

        for p in out["paragraphs"]: 

            if isinstance(p, dict): 

                p2 = dict(p) 

                if isinstance(p2.get("text"), str): 

                    p2["text"] = m(p2["text"]) 

                new_paras.append(p2) 

        out["paragraphs"] = new_paras 

 

    if "slides" in out and isinstance(out["slides"], list): 

        new_slides = [] 

        for s in out["slides"]: 

            if isinstance(s, dict): 

                s2 = dict(s) 

                if isinstance(s2.get("text"), str): 

                    s2["text"] = m(s2["text"]) 

                new_slides.append(s2) 

        out["slides"] = new_slides 

 

    if "sheets" in out and isinstance(out["sheets"], dict): 

        new_sheets = {} 

        for name, sheet in out["sheets"].items(): 

            if not isinstance(sheet, dict): 

                continue 

            sh2 = dict(sheet) 

            if isinstance(sh2.get("header"), list): 

                sh2["header"] = [m(str(x)) for x in sh2["header"]] 

            if isinstance(sh2.get("rows"), list): 

                masked_rows = [] 

                for r in sh2["rows"]: 

                    if isinstance(r, list): 

                        masked_rows.append([m(str(x)) for x in r]) 

                sh2["rows"] = masked_rows 

            new_sheets[str(name)] = sh2 

        out["sheets"] = new_sheets 

 

    return out 

 

 

@app.post("/compare/stream") 

async def compare_stream( 

    request: Request, 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

    file_a: UploadFile = File(...), 

    file_b: UploadFile = File(...), 

): 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

    rid = request.state.request_id 

 

    async def gen(): 

        yield sse_event("start", json.dumps({"request_id": rid}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

        try: 

            a_doc = await parse_upload(file_a) 

            b_doc = await parse_upload(file_b) 

            request.state.file_a_sha256 = a_doc.sha256 

            request.state.file_b_sha256 = b_doc.sha256 

 

            a_doc.parsed = _mask_parsed(settings, a_doc.parsed) 

            b_doc.parsed = _mask_parsed(settings, b_doc.parsed) 

            yield sse_event("parsed", json.dumps({"a_type": a_doc.doc_type, "b_type": b_doc.doc_type}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

 

            diff_payload = _compute_diff(settings, a_doc, b_doc) 

            yield sse_event("diffed", json.dumps({"mode": diff_payload.mode}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

 

            file_meta_a = _file_meta(a_doc) 

            file_meta_b = _file_meta(b_doc) 

            prompt = _build_llm_prompt(diff_payload, file_meta_a, file_meta_b, settings.MAX_TEXT_CHARS_FOR_LLM) 

            yield sse_event("llm_prompt_ready", json.dumps({"chars": len(prompt)}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

 

            llm_summary = None 

            semantic_hit = False 

            embedding = None 

 

            key_parts = { 

                "a": a_doc.sha256, 

                "b": b_doc.sha256, 

                "types": [a_doc.doc_type, b_doc.doc_type], 

                "max_items": settings.MAX_DIFF_ITEMS, 

                "pii_cfg": settings.pii_config(), 

                "model": settings.OPENAI_MODEL, 

                "embed_model": settings.OPENAI_EMBEDDING_MODEL, 

                "max_tokens": settings.OPENAI_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS, 

                "threshold": settings.SEMANTIC_CACHE_THRESHOLD, 

            } 

            exact_key = stable_compact_hash(key_parts) 

            cached = request.app.state.exact_cache.get(exact_key) 

            if cached: 

                resp: CompareResponse = cached 

                resp2 = resp.model_copy(deep=True) 

                resp2.request_id = rid 

                resp2.exact_cache_hit = True 

                request.state.exact_cache_hit = True 

                request.state.semantic_cache_hit = getattr(resp, "semantic_cache_hit", False) 

                request.state.compare_id = resp2.compare_id 

                yield sse_event("cache_hit", json.dumps({"exact": True}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

                yield sse_event("result", resp2.model_dump_json()) 

                yield sse_event("done", json.dumps({"ok": True}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

                return 

 

            try: 

                llm = _make_llm(settings) 

                try: 

                    emb_key = embedding_cache_key(settings.OPENAI_EMBEDDING_MODEL, prompt) 

                    embedding = request.app.state.embedding_cache.get(emb_key) 

                    if embedding is None: 

                        embedding = llm.embed(prompt) 

                        request.app.state.embedding_cache.set(emb_key, embedding) 

 

                    semantic_hit, semantic_val = request.app.state.semantic_cache.lookup(embedding) 

                    if semantic_hit and semantic_val: 

                        resp_prev: CompareResponse = semantic_val 

                        resp_prev2 = resp_prev.model_copy(deep=True) 

                        resp_prev2.request_id = rid 

                        resp_prev2.semantic_cache_hit = True 

                        resp_prev2.summary.cache_hit = True 

                        request.state.semantic_cache_hit = True 

                        request.state.compare_id = resp_prev2.compare_id 

                        request.app.state.exact_cache.set(exact_key, resp_prev2.model_copy(deep=True)) 

                        yield sse_event("cache_hit", json.dumps({"semantic": True}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

                        yield sse_event("result", resp_prev2.model_dump_json()) 

                        yield sse_event("done", json.dumps({"ok": True}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

                        return 

 

                    llm_summary = llm.summarize(prompt) 

                finally: 

                    llm.close() 

            except Exception: 

                llm_summary = _offline_summary(diff_payload) 

 

            compare_id = str(uuid.uuid4()) 

            request.state.compare_id = compare_id 

 

            resp = CompareResponse( 

                request_id=rid, 

                compare_id=compare_id, 

                file_a=file_meta_a, 

                file_b=file_meta_b, 

                diff=diff_payload, 

                summary=llm_summary, 

                exact_cache_hit=False, 

                semantic_cache_hit=bool(semantic_hit), 

            ) 

 

            request.app.state.stores.put_compare(compare_id, resp) 

            request.app.state.exact_cache.set(exact_key, resp) 

 

            if embedding and not semantic_hit: 

                hint = stable_compact_hash({"a": a_doc.sha256, "b": b_doc.sha256, "mode": diff_payload.mode, "t": time.time()}) 

                request.app.state.semantic_cache.insert(embedding=embedding, value=resp, key_hint=hint) 

 

            yield sse_event("result", resp.model_dump_json()) 

            yield sse_event("done", json.dumps({"ok": True}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

        except HTTPException as he: 

            err = ErrorResponse(request_id=rid, error="http_error", detail=str(he.detail)) 

            yield sse_event("error", err.model_dump_json()) 

        except Exception as e: 

            err = ErrorResponse(request_id=rid, error="internal_error", detail="Internal error") 

            yield sse_event("error", err.model_dump_json()) 

 

    return StreamingResponse(gen(), media_type="text/event-stream", headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache"}) 

 

 

@app.get("/export/summary", response_model=ExportSummaryResponse) 

async def export_summary( 

    request: Request, 

    compare_id: str, 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

): 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

    rid = request.state.request_id 

 

    rec = request.app.state.stores.get_compare(compare_id) 

    if not rec: 

        raise HTTPException(status_code=HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="compare_id not found or expired") 

 

    # Response is already masked; ensure summary fields are masked again defensively (idempotent). 

    cfg = settings.pii_config() 

    salt = settings.PII_HASH_SALT 

    s = rec.summary.model_copy(deep=True) 

    s.overview = mask_text(s.overview, cfg, salt) 

    s.key_changes = [mask_text(x, cfg, salt) for x in s.key_changes] 

    s.risk_notes = [mask_text(x, cfg, salt) for x in s.risk_notes] 

    out = ExportSummaryResponse( 

        request_id=rid, 

        compare_id=compare_id, 

        file_a=rec.file_a, 

        file_b=rec.file_b, 

        summary=s, 

    ) 

    return out 

 

 

@app.post("/feedback", response_model=FeedbackResponse) 

async def feedback( 

    request: Request, 

    body: FeedbackRequest, 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

): 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

    rid = request.state.request_id 

 

    # Mask PII in feedback before storage/logging. 

    cfg = settings.pii_config() 

    salt = settings.PII_HASH_SALT 

    body.comment = mask_text(body.comment or "", cfg, salt) 

    # Also mask any string metadata values defensively 

    safe_meta: dict[str, Any] = {} 

    for k, v in (body.metadata or {}).items(): 

        if isinstance(v, str): 

            safe_meta[str(k)] = mask_text(v, cfg, salt) 

        else: 

            safe_meta[str(k)] = v 

    body.metadata = safe_meta 

 

    request.app.state.stores.put_feedback(rid, body)

Ui/config.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import os 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

 

 

@dataclass(frozen=True) 

class UIConfig: 

    backend_url: str 

    api_key: str 

    request_timeout_s: float 

 

    @staticmethod 

    def from_env() -> "UIConfig": 

        backend_url = os.getenv("BACKEND_URL", "http://localhost:8000").strip().rstrip("/") 

        api_key = os.getenv("API_KEY", "").strip() 

        timeout = float(os.getenv("UI_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_SECONDS", "120")) 

        return UIConfig(backend_url=backend_url, api_key=api_key, request_timeout_s=timeout) 

 

 

ui/api_client.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import json 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

from typing import Dict, Generator, Optional, Tuple 

 

import httpx 

 

 

@dataclass(frozen=True) 

class SSEEvent: 

    event: str 

    data: str 

 

 

def _headers(api_key: str, request_id: str | None = None) -> dict: 

    h = {"Accept": "application/json"} 

    if api_key: 

        h["X-API-Key"] = api_key 

    if request_id: 

        h["X-Request-Id"] = request_id 

    return h 

 

 

def compare( 

    backend_url: str, 

    api_key: str, 

    file_a_name: str, 

    file_a_bytes: bytes, 

    file_a_type: str, 

    file_b_name: str, 

    file_b_bytes: bytes, 

    file_b_type: str, 

    request_timeout_s: float, 

    request_id: str | None = None, 

) -> dict: 

    url = f"{backend_url}/compare" 

    files = { 

        "file_a": (file_a_name, file_a_bytes, file_a_type or "application/octet-stream"), 

        "file_b": (file_b_name, file_b_bytes, file_b_type or "application/octet-stream"), 

    } 

    with httpx.Client(timeout=httpx.Timeout(request_timeout_s), follow_redirects=False) as client: 

        r = client.post(url, files=files, headers=_headers(api_key, request_id)) 

        r.raise_for_status() 

        return r.json() 

 

 

def export_summary( 

    backend_url: str, 

    api_key: str, 

    compare_id: str, 

    request_timeout_s: float, 

    request_id: str | None = None, 

) -> dict: 

    url = f"{backend_url}/export/summary" 

    params = {"compare_id": compare_id} 

    with httpx.Client(timeout=httpx.Timeout(request_timeout_s), follow_redirects=False) as client: 

        r = client.get(url, params=params, headers=_headers(api_key, request_id)) 

        r.raise_for_status() 

        return r.json() 

 

 

def _iter_sse_lines(resp: httpx.Response) -> Generator[str, None, None]: 

    for line in resp.iter_lines(): 

        if line is None: 

            continue 

        yield line 

 

 

def compare_stream( 

    backend_url: str, 

    api_key: str, 

    file_a_name: str, 

    file_a_bytes: bytes, 

    file_a_type: str, 

    file_b_name: str, 

    file_b_bytes: bytes, 

    file_b_type: str, 

    request_timeout_s: float, 

    request_id: str | None = None, 

) -> Generator[SSEEvent, None, None]: 

    url = f"{backend_url}/compare/stream" 

    files = { 

        "file_a": (file_a_name, file_a_bytes, file_a_type or "application/octet-stream"), 

        "file_b": (file_b_name, file_b_bytes, file_b_type or "application/octet-stream"), 

    } 

    headers = _headers(api_key, request_id) 

    headers["Accept"] = "text/event-stream" 

 

    with httpx.Client(timeout=httpx.Timeout(request_timeout_s), follow_redirects=False) as client: 

        with client.stream("POST", url, files=files, headers=headers) as resp: 

            resp.raise_for_status() 

            ev_name = "message" 

            data_lines: list[str] = [] 

 

            for raw in _iter_sse_lines(resp): 

                if raw == "": 

                    if data_lines: 

                        yield SSEEvent(event=ev_name, data="\n".join(data_lines)) 

                    ev_name = "message" 

                    data_lines = [] 

                    continue 

 

                if raw.startswith("event:"): 

                    ev_name = raw.split(":", 1)[1].strip() or "message" 

                elif raw.startswith("data:"): 

                    data_lines.append(raw.split(":", 1)[1].lstrip()) 

                else: 

                    # ignore comments/unknown lines per SSE spec 

                    continue 

ui/a11y.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import streamlit as st 

 

 

def apply_high_contrast(enabled: bool) -> None: 

    if not enabled: 

        st.markdown( 

            """ 

<style> 

/* Keep minimal: preserve Streamlit defaults */ 

</style> 

""", 

            unsafe_allow_html=True, 

        ) 

        return 

 

    st.markdown( 

        """ 

<style> 

:root { 

  --hc-bg: #0b0f14; 

  --hc-fg: #f8fafc; 

  --hc-ac: #22c55e; 

  --hc-warn: #f59e0b; 

  --hc-bad: #ef4444; 

  --hc-card: #111827; 

} 

html, body, [data-testid="stAppViewContainer"] { background: var(--hc-bg) !important; color: var(--hc-fg) !important; } 

[data-testid="stSidebar"] { background: #0f172a !important; } 

[data-testid="stMarkdownContainer"] p, [data-testid="stMarkdownContainer"] li, [data-testid="stMarkdownContainer"] span, 

label, .stTextInput label, .stSelectbox label, .stFileUploader label { color: var(--hc-fg) !important; } 

.stButton button, .stDownloadButton button { 

  border: 2px solid var(--hc-ac) !important; 

  background: transparent !important; 

  color: var(--hc-fg) !important; 

  border-radius: 12px !important; 

} 

.stButton button:focus, .stDownloadButton button:focus { outline: 3px solid #93c5fd !important; outline-offset: 2px !important; } 

div[data-testid="stToast"] { color: var(--hc-fg) !important; } 

code { color: #eab308 !important; } 

</style> 

""", 

        unsafe_allow_html=True, 

    ) 

 

 

def aria_hint(text: str) -> None: 

    # Streamlit doesn't expose ARIA attrs for all widgets; provide assistive hints near controls. 

    st.markdown( 

        f""" 

<div role="note" aria-label="Accessibility hint" style="font-size:0.9rem; opacity:0.9;"> 

{text} 

</div> 

""", 

        unsafe_allow_html=True, 

    ) 

ui/diff_view.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import json 

from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple 

 

import streamlit as st 

 

 

_KIND_COLORS = { 

    "added": ("#064e3b", "#34d399"), 

    "removed": ("#7f1d1d", "#fb7185"), 

    "modified": ("#1e3a8a", "#60a5fa"), 

} 

 

 

def _badge(kind: str) -> str: 

    bg, fg = _KIND_COLORS.get(kind, ("#111827", "#e5e7eb")) 

    return f""" 

<span style="display:inline-block;padding:2px 10px;border-radius:999px;background:{bg};color:{fg};font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:600;"> 

{kind.upper()} 

</span> 

""" 

 

 

def _panel(title: str, body: str, kind: str) -> None: 

    bg, fg = _KIND_COLORS.get(kind, ("#0f172a", "#e5e7eb")) 

    st.markdown( 

        f""" 

<div style="border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,0.25); border-left:6px solid {fg}; 

            background:rgba(15,23,42,0.25); border-radius:16px; padding:12px 14px; margin-bottom:10px;"> 

  <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;"> 

    <div style="font-weight:700;">{title}</div> 

    {_badge(kind)} 

  </div> 

  <pre style="white-space:pre-wrap; word-wrap:break-word; margin:10px 0 0 0; color:#e5e7eb;">{body}</pre> 

</div> 

""", 

        unsafe_allow_html=True, 

    ) 

 

 

def _clip(s: str, limit: int = 1800) -> str: 

    if s is None: 

        return "" 

    s = str(s) 

    if len(s) <= limit: 

        return s 

    return s[: limit - 12] + "…(truncated)" 

 

 

def _filter_items(items: list[dict], kinds: set[str]) -> list[dict]: 

    return [i for i in items if i.get("kind") in kinds] 

 

 

def render_diff(resp: dict, filter_kinds: set[str]) -> None: 

    diff = (resp or {}).get("diff") or {} 

    mode = diff.get("mode") 

 

    if mode == "text": 

        td = diff.get("text") or {} 

        _render_text_like("Text diff", td, filter_kinds) 

        return 

 

    if mode == "docx": 

        dd = diff.get("docx") or {} 

        _render_text_like("DOCX paragraph diff", dd, filter_kinds) 

        return 

 

    if mode == "tabular": 

        td = diff.get("tabular") or {} 

        _render_tabular(td) 

        return 

 

    if mode == "pdf": 

        pd = diff.get("pdf") or {} 

        _render_pdf(pd, filter_kinds) 

        return 

 

    st.warning("No diff to display.") 

 

 

def _render_text_like(title: str, payload: dict, filter_kinds: set[str]) -> None: 

    items = payload.get("items") or [] 

    items = _filter_items(items, filter_kinds) 

 

    st.markdown(f"### {title}") 

    counts = { 

        "added": int(payload.get("added") or 0), 

        "removed": int(payload.get("removed") or 0), 

        "modified": int(payload.get("modified") or 0), 

    } 

    st.caption(f"Added: {counts['added']} · Removed: {counts['removed']} · Modified: {counts['modified']}") 

 

    if not items: 

        st.info("No changes match the current filter.") 

        return 

 

    col_l, col_r = st.columns(2, gap="large") 

    for idx, it in enumerate(items, start=1): 

        kind = it.get("kind") or "modified" 

        ref = it.get("ref") or f"item {idx}" 

        before = _clip(it.get("before") or "") 

        after = _clip(it.get("after") or "") 

 

        with col_l: 

            _panel(f"{ref} · BEFORE", before if before else "(empty)", kind if kind != "added" else "removed") 

        with col_r: 

            _panel(f"{ref} · AFTER", after if after else "(empty)", kind if kind != "removed" else "added") 

 

 

def _render_tabular(payload: dict) -> None: 

    st.markdown("### Tabular diff (CSV/XLSX)") 

    schema = payload.get("schema") or {} 

    st.markdown("#### Schema changes") 

    cols = st.columns(3) 

    with cols[0]: 

        st.write("Added columns") 

        st.code("\n".join(schema.get("added_columns") or []) or "(none)") 

    with cols[1]: 

        st.write("Removed columns") 

        st.code("\n".join(schema.get("removed_columns") or []) or "(none)") 

    with cols[2]: 

        st.write("Sheets") 

        added_s = schema.get("added_sheets") or [] 

        removed_s = schema.get("removed_sheets") or [] 

        st.code(f"Added: {', '.join(added_s) if added_s else '(none)'}\nRemoved: {', '.join(removed_s) if removed_s else '(none)'}") 

 

    st.markdown("#### Cell changes") 

    cell_changes = payload.get("cell_changes") or [] 

    if not cell_changes: 

        st.info("No cell-level changes detected (or within intersection bounds).") 

        return 

    st.dataframe(cell_changes, use_container_width=True, hide_index=True) 

 

 

def _render_pdf(payload: dict, filter_kinds: set[str]) -> None: 

    st.markdown("### PDF diff (best-effort with page references)") 

    pages = payload.get("pages") or [] 

    if not pages: 

        st.info("No page diffs available.") 

        return 

 

    for p in pages: 

        page_num = p.get("page") 

        td = (p.get("diff") or {}) 

        items = _filter_items(td.get("items") or [], filter_kinds) 

 

        with st.expander(f"Page {page_num} · Added {td.get('added', 0)} · Removed {td.get('removed', 0)} · Modified {td.get('modified', 0)}", expanded=False): 

            if not items: 

                st.caption("No changes match the current filter for this page.") 

                continue 

            for idx, it in enumerate(items, start=1): 

                kind = it.get("kind") or "modified" 

                ref = it.get("ref") or f"page {page_num} item {idx}" 

                before = _clip(it.get("before") or "") 

                after = _clip(it.get("after") or "") 

                c1, c2 = st.columns(2, gap="large") 

                with c1: 

                    _panel(f"{ref} · BEFORE", before if before else "(empty)", kind if kind != "added" else "removed") 

                with c2: 

                    _panel(f"{ref} · AFTER", after if after else "(empty)", kind if kind != "removed" else "added") 

 

 

def render_json(resp: dict) -> None: 

    st.markdown("### Machine diff JSON") 

    st.json(resp, expanded=False) 

ui/voice.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import io 

import tempfile 

from typing import Optional 

 

import streamlit as st 

 

 

def browser_tts(text: str, rate: float = 1.0, pitch: float = 1.0) -> None: 

    safe = (text or "").replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("`", "\\`").replace("${", "\\${") 

    st.components.v1.html( 

        f""" 

<div aria-label="Text to speech output" role="region"></div> 

<script> 

try {{ 

  const msg = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(`{safe}`); 

  msg.rate = {rate}; 

  msg.pitch = {pitch}; 

  window.speechSynthesis.cancel(); 

  window.speechSynthesis.speak(msg); 

}} catch (e) {{ 

  // best-effort: ignore 

}} 

</script> 

""", 

        height=0, 

    ) 

 

 

def transcribe_audio_bytes(audio_bytes: bytes) -> Optional[str]: 

    """ 

    Best-effort STT: 

    - If SpeechRecognition + pocketsphinx/google available, use it. 

    - If not installed, return None (no crash). 

    """ 

    try: 

        import speech_recognition as sr  # type: ignore 

    except Exception: 

        return None 

 

    try: 

        r = sr.Recognizer() 

        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".wav", delete=True) as f: 

            f.write(audio_bytes) 

            f.flush() 

            with sr.AudioFile(f.name) as source: 

                audio = r.record(source) 

        # Prefer offline if available; fallback to google if network is allowed in environment. 

        try: 

            return r.recognize_sphinx(audio) 

        except Exception: 

            try: 

                return r.recognize_google(audio) 

            except Exception: 

                return None 

    except Exception: 

        return None 

 

 

def tts_audio_bytes(text: str) -> Optional[bytes]: 

    """ 

    Best-effort server-side TTS: 

    - If pyttsx3 available, generate WAV bytes. 

    - If not installed, return None. 

    """ 

    try: 

        import pyttsx3  # type: ignore 

    except Exception: 

        return None 

 

    try: 

        engine = pyttsx3.init() 

        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".wav", delete=True) as f: 

            engine.save_to_file(text or "", f.name) 

            engine.runAndWait() 

            f.seek(0) 

            return f.read() 

    except Exception: 

        return None

if "last_response" not in st.session_state: 

    st.session_state.last_response = None 

if "last_error" not in st.session_state:

    )

st.markdown("## Document Comparison AI Agent") 

st.caption("Upload two documents, compare, view diffs side-by-side, inspect JSON, and export the summary.") 

 

colA, colB = st.columns(2, gap="large") 

with colA: 

    file_a = st.file_uploader( 

        "Upload document A (pdf, txt, csv, docx, xlsx, pptx)", 

        type=["pdf", "txt", "csv", "docx", "xlsx", "pptx"],

        help="Document A (legacy / baseline).", 

    ) 

with colB: 

    file_b = st.file_uploader( 

        "Upload document B (pdf, txt, csv, docx, xlsx, pptx)", 

        type=["pdf", "txt", "csv", "docx", "xlsx", "pptx"], 

        key="file_b", 

        help="Document B (modern / updated).", 

    ) 

 

st.divider() 

 

filters_col, actions_col = st.columns([2, 3], gap="large") 

with filters_col:

        "Show changes",

        default=["added", "removed", "modified"],

    )

with actions_col:

    compare_btn = c1.button("Compare (fast)", type="primary", use_container_width=True, help="Calls /compare and renders the full result.") 

    stream_btn = c2.button("Compare (stream)", use_container_width=True, help="Calls /compare/stream and shows progress events.")
