backend/app/agents/state.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from typing import TypedDict, Literal, Any 

 

 

UserRole = Literal[ 

    "policy_user", 

    "compliance_expert", 

    "federal_officer", 

    "audit", 

] 

 

AgentName = Literal[ 

    "policy_agent", 

    "compliance_agent", 

    "federal_agent", 

    "audit_agent", 

] 

 

 

class AgentState(TypedDict, total=False): 

    request_id: str 

    user_id: str 

    user_role: UserRole 

    selected_agent: AgentName 

 

    # org / auth context 

    manager: str | None 

    skip_manager: str | None 

    org_scope_allowed: bool 

    access_scope: Literal["self", "team", "org", "audit"] 

 

    # business input 

    query: str 

    regulator: str | None 

    compare_response: dict[str, Any] 

 

    # working state 

    evidence: list[dict[str, Any]] 

    blocked: bool 

    block_reason: str | None 

 

    # final output 

    final_prompt: str 

    final_response: dict[str, Any] 

backend/app/agents/roles.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from typing import Literal 

 

 

UserRole = Literal[ 

    "policy_user", 

    "compliance_expert", 

    "federal_officer", 

    "audit", 

] 

 

 

def map_role_to_agent(role: UserRole) -> str: 

    if role == "policy_user": 

        return "policy_agent" 

    if role == "compliance_expert": 

        return "compliance_agent" 

    if role == "federal_officer": 

        return "federal_agent" 

    return "audit_agent" 

backend/app/agents/prompts.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from typing import Any 

 

 

BASE_RULES = """ 

You must use ONLY the provided evidence and machine diff. 

Do not guess. 

Do not add unsupported claims. 

Return structured JSON only. 

""" 

 

 

def build_role_prompt(role: str, query: str, evidence: list[dict[str, Any]], compare_response: dict[str, Any]) -> str: 

    if role == "policy_user": 

        role_instruction = """ 

Explain changes in simple business language. 

Focus on summary, key changes, and user impact. 

Avoid technical metadata unless necessary. 

""" 

    elif role == "compliance_expert": 

        role_instruction = """ 

Provide detailed compliance analysis. 

Highlight risk notes, affected clauses, and operational implications. 

Use clear evidence-backed reasoning. 

""" 

    elif role == "federal_officer": 

        role_instruction = """ 

Strict regulatory mode. 

Return only compliance-relevant findings. 

Every material statement must be evidence-backed. 

No assumptions and no speculative interpretation. 

""" 

    else: 

        role_instruction = """ 

Audit mode. 

Return full transparency: 

- summary 

- evidence references 

- response metadata 

- reasoning trace summary 

""" 

 

    return f""" 

{BASE_RULES} 

 

ROLE: 

{role} 

 

ROLE INSTRUCTION: 

{role_instruction} 

 

USER QUERY: 

{query} 

 

MACHINE COMPARE RESPONSE: 

{compare_response} 

 

EVIDENCE: 

{evidence} 

""" 

backend/app/agents/nodes.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from typing import Any 

 

from .roles import map_role_to_agent 

from .prompts import build_role_prompt 

 

 

def select_agent_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    role = state.get("user_role", "policy_user") 

    return {"selected_agent": map_role_to_agent(role)} 

 

 

def policy_agent_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    prompt = build_role_prompt( 

        role="policy_user", 

        query=state.get("query", ""), 

        evidence=state.get("evidence", []), 

        compare_response=state.get("compare_response", {}), 

    ) 

    return {"final_prompt": prompt} 

 

 

def compliance_agent_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    prompt = build_role_prompt( 

        role="compliance_expert", 

        query=state.get("query", ""), 

        evidence=state.get("evidence", []), 

        compare_response=state.get("compare_response", {}), 

    ) 

    return {"final_prompt": prompt} 

 

 

def federal_agent_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    prompt = build_role_prompt( 

        role="federal_officer", 

        query=state.get("query", ""), 

        evidence=state.get("evidence", []), 

        compare_response=state.get("compare_response", {}), 

    ) 

    return {"final_prompt": prompt} 

 

 

def audit_agent_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    prompt = build_role_prompt( 

        role="audit", 

        query=state.get("query", ""), 

        evidence=state.get("evidence", []), 

        compare_response=state.get("compare_response", {}), 

    ) 

    return {"final_prompt": prompt} 

 

 

def finalize_response_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    """ 

    Replace this with your deterministic OpenAI call + schema validation. 

    Here we just shape a role-specific response shell. 

    """ 

    role = state.get("user_role", "policy_user") 

    compare_response = state.get("compare_response", {}) 

    summary = (compare_response or {}).get("summary", {}) 

    diff = (compare_response or {}).get("diff", {}) 

 

    if role == "policy_user": 

        result = { 

            "view": "policy_user", 

            "summary": summary.get("overview", ""), 

            "key_changes": summary.get("key_changes", []), 

        } 

 

    elif role == "compliance_expert": 

        result = { 

            "view": "compliance_expert", 

            "summary": summary, 

            "diff": diff, 

            "risk_notes": summary.get("risk_notes", []), 

        } 

 

    elif role == "federal_officer": 

        result = { 

            "view": "federal_officer", 

            "summary": summary.get("overview", ""), 

            "risk_notes": summary.get("risk_notes", []), 

            "evidence_required": True, 

            "diff": diff, 

        } 

 

    else: 

        result = { 

            "view": "audit", 

            "summary": summary, 

            "diff": diff, 

            "metadata": { 

                "request_id": compare_response.get("request_id"), 

                "compare_id": compare_response.get("compare_id"), 

                "exact_cache_hit": compare_response.get("exact_cache_hit"), 

                "semantic_cache_hit": compare_response.get("semantic_cache_hit"), 

            }, 

        } 

 

    return {"final_response": result} 

backend/app/agents/graph.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from langgraph.graph import StateGraph, END 

 

from .state import AgentState 

from .nodes import ( 

    select_agent_node, 

    policy_agent_node, 

    compliance_agent_node, 

    federal_agent_node, 

    audit_agent_node, 

    finalize_response_node, 

) 

 

 

def build_role_graph(): 

    graph = StateGraph(AgentState) 

 

    graph.add_node("select_agent", select_agent_node) 

    graph.add_node("policy_agent", policy_agent_node) 

    graph.add_node("compliance_agent", compliance_agent_node) 

    graph.add_node("federal_agent", federal_agent_node) 

    graph.add_node("audit_agent", audit_agent_node) 

    graph.add_node("finalize", finalize_response_node) 

 

    graph.set_entry_point("select_agent") 

 

    def route_after_select(state: dict) -> str: 

        selected = state.get("selected_agent") 

        if selected == "policy_agent": 

            return "policy_agent" 

        if selected == "compliance_agent": 

            return "compliance_agent" 

        if selected == "federal_agent": 

            return "federal_agent" 

        return "audit_agent" 

 

    graph.add_conditional_edges( 

        "select_agent", 

        route_after_select, 

        { 

            "policy_agent": "policy_agent", 

            "compliance_agent": "compliance_agent", 

            "federal_agent": "federal_agent", 

            "audit_agent": "audit_agent", 

        }, 

    ) 

 

    graph.add_edge("policy_agent", "finalize") 

    graph.add_edge("compliance_agent", "finalize") 

    graph.add_edge("federal_agent", "finalize") 

    graph.add_edge("audit_agent", "finalize") 

 

    graph.add_edge("finalize", END) 

 

    return graph.compile() 

Suggested rules 

Policy User 

Access scope = self 

Can only view their own compare jobs 

Compliance Expert 

Access scope = team 

Can view self + direct team scope 

Federal Compliance Officer 

Access scope = org 

Can view cross-team compliance evidence 

Audit Team 

Access scope = audit 

Can view trace, metadata, and audit logs 

backend/app/security/org_permissions.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from typing import Literal 

 

 

AccessScope = Literal["self", "team", "org", "audit"] 

 

 

def role_to_scope(role: str) -> AccessScope: 

    if role == "policy_user": 

        return "self" 

    if role == "compliance_expert": 

        return "team" 

    if role == "federal_officer": 

        return "org" 

    return "audit" 

 

 

def can_access_compare( 

    requester_role: str, 

    requester_id: str, 

    owner_id: str, 

    owner_manager_id: str | None = None, 

    requester_manager_id: str | None = None, 

) -> bool: 

    scope = role_to_scope(requester_role) 

 

    if scope == "audit": 

        return True 

 

    if scope == "org": 

        return True 

 

    if scope == "team": 

        # same manager => same team 

        return ( 

            requester_id == owner_id 

            or ( 

                requester_manager_id 

                and owner_manager_id 

                and requester_manager_id == owner_manager_id 

            ) 

        ) 

 

    # self 

    return requester_id == owner_id 

Optional manager-chain helper 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

def manager_chain_contains(target_manager: str | None, chain: list[str]) -> bool: 

    if not target_manager: 

        return False 

    return target_manager in chain

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from fastapi import Header 

 

 

def get_request_context( 

    x_user_id: str = Header(..., alias="X-User-Id"), 

    x_user_role: str = Header("policy_user", alias="X-User-Role"), 

    x_manager_id: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="X-Manager-Id"), 

    x_skip_manager_id: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="X-Skip-Manager-Id"), 

): 

    return { 

        "user_id": x_user_id, 

        "user_role": x_user_role, 

        "manager": x_manager_id, 

        "skip_manager": x_skip_manager_id, 

    } 

4) Use LangGraph + org permissions together 

Example in /compare 

from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException 

 

from backend.app.agents.graph import build_role_graph 

from backend.app.security.context import get_request_context 

from backend.app.security.org_permissions import can_access_compare 

 

 

graph = build_role_graph() 

 

 

@app.post("/compare/role-aware") 

async def compare_role_aware( 

    body: dict, 

    ctx: dict = Depends(get_request_context), 

): 

    compare_owner_id = body.get("owner_id", ctx["user_id"]) 

    compare_owner_manager_id = body.get("owner_manager_id", ctx.get("manager")) 

 

    allowed = can_access_compare( 

        requester_role=ctx["user_role"], 

        requester_id=ctx["user_id"], 

        owner_id=compare_owner_id, 

        owner_manager_id=compare_owner_manager_id, 

        requester_manager_id=ctx.get("manager"), 

    ) 

 

    if not allowed: 

        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not allowed for this org scope") 

 

    state = { 

        "request_id": body.get("request_id"), 

        "user_id": ctx["user_id"], 

        "user_role": ctx["user_role"], 

        "manager": ctx.get("manager"), 

        "skip_manager": ctx.get("skip_manager"), 

        "query": body.get("query", ""), 

        "compare_response": body.get("compare_response", {}), 

        "evidence": body.get("evidence", []), 

    } 

 

    result = graph.invoke(state) 

    return result["final_response"]

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from typing import Any 

 

 

BASE_RULES = """ 

You must use ONLY the compare response and evidence provided. 

Do not guess. 

Do not add unsupported claims. 

Return structured JSON only. 

""" 

 

 

def build_role_prompt( 

    role: str, 

    query: str, 

    compare_response: dict[str, Any], 

    evidence: list[dict[str, Any]], 

) -> str: 

    role = (role or "policy_user").lower().strip() 

 

    if role == "policy_user": 

        instruction = """ 

Explain document changes in simple business language. 

Focus on summary, key changes, and direct user impact. 

Avoid technical metadata unless critical. 

""" 

    elif role == "compliance_expert": 

        instruction = """ 

Provide detailed compliance analysis. 

Highlight affected clauses, operational implications, and risk notes. 

Keep the response evidence-backed and concise. 

""" 

    elif role == "federal_officer": 

        instruction = """ 

Strict regulatory mode. 

Return only compliance-relevant, evidence-backed findings. 

No speculation, no assumptions, no unsupported interpretation. 

""" 

    else: 

        instruction = """ 

Audit mode. 

Return full transparency with: 

- summary 

- evidence references 

- metadata 

- reasoning trace summary 

""" 

 

    return f""" 

{BASE_RULES} 

 

ROLE: 

{role} 

 

ROLE INSTRUCTION: 

{instruction} 

 

USER QUERY: 

{query} 

 

COMPARE RESPONSE: 

{compare_response} 

 

EVIDENCE: 

{evidence} 

"""

backend/app/agents/formatter.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from typing import Any 

 

 

def _summary(resp: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]: 

    return dict((resp or {}).get("summary") or {}) 

 

 

def _diff(resp: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]: 

    return dict((resp or {}).get("diff") or {}) 

 

 

def format_response_for_role(resp: dict[str, Any], role: str) -> dict[str, Any]: 

    """ 

    Final response filter: 

    - policy_user        -> concise 

    - compliance_expert  -> detailed 

    - federal_officer    -> regulatory/evidence focused 

    - audit              -> full transparency 

    """ 

    role = (role or "policy_user").lower().strip() 

    s = _summary(resp) 

    d = _diff(resp) 

 

    if role == "policy_user": 

        return { 

            "view": "policy_user", 

            "request_id": resp.get("request_id"), 

            "compare_id": resp.get("compare_id"), 

            "summary": s.get("overview", ""), 

            "key_changes": s.get("key_changes", []), 

        } 

 

    if role == "compliance_expert": 

        return { 

            "view": "compliance_expert", 

            "request_id": resp.get("request_id"), 

            "compare_id": resp.get("compare_id"), 

            "summary": s, 

            "diff": d, 

            "risk_notes": s.get("risk_notes", []), 

        } 

 

    if role == "federal_officer": 

        return { 

            "view": "federal_officer", 

            "request_id": resp.get("request_id"), 

            "compare_id": resp.get("compare_id"), 

            "summary": s.get("overview", ""), 

            "risk_notes": s.get("risk_notes", []), 

            "evidence_required": True, 

            "diff": d, 

        } 

 

    return { 

        "view": "audit", 

        "request_id": resp.get("request_id"), 

        "compare_id": resp.get("compare_id"), 

        "summary": s, 

        "diff": d, 

        "metadata": { 

            "exact_cache_hit": resp.get("exact_cache_hit"), 

            "semantic_cache_hit": resp.get("semantic_cache_hit"), 

            "file_a": resp.get("file_a"), 

            "file_b": resp.get("file_b"), 

        }, 

    }

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from .prompts import build_role_prompt 

from .roles import role_to_agent 

from .formatter import format_response_for_role 

 

 

def supervisor_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    role = state.get("user_role", "policy_user") 

    return { 

        "selected_agent": role_to_agent(role), 

    } 

 

 

def policy_agent_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    prompt = build_role_prompt( 

        role="policy_user", 

        query=state.get("query", ""), 

        compare_response=state.get("compare_response", {}), 

        evidence=state.get("evidence", []), 

    ) 

    return {"final_prompt": prompt} 

 

 

def compliance_agent_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    prompt = build_role_prompt( 

        role="compliance_expert", 

        query=state.get("query", ""), 

        compare_response=state.get("compare_response", {}), 

        evidence=state.get("evidence", []), 

    ) 

    return {"final_prompt": prompt} 

 

 

def federal_agent_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    prompt = build_role_prompt( 

        role="federal_officer", 

        query=state.get("query", ""), 

        compare_response=state.get("compare_response", {}), 

        evidence=state.get("evidence", []), 

    ) 

    return {"final_prompt": prompt} 

 

 

def audit_agent_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    prompt = build_role_prompt( 

        role="audit", 

        query=state.get("query", ""), 

        compare_response=state.get("compare_response", {}), 

        evidence=state.get("evidence", []), 

    ) 

    return {"final_prompt": prompt} 

 

 

def finalize_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    """ 

    Final formatter node. 

    If later you want LLM-per-role, plug it here before formatting. 

    """ 

    role = state.get("user_role", "policy_user") 

    compare_response = state.get("compare_response", {}) 

    return { 

        "final_response": format_response_for_role(compare_response, role) 

    }

backend/app/agents/graph.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from langgraph.graph import StateGraph, END 

 

from .state import AgentState 

from .nodes import ( 

    supervisor_node, 

    policy_agent_node, 

    compliance_agent_node, 

    federal_agent_node, 

    audit_agent_node, 

    finalize_node, 

) 

 

 

def build_role_graph(): 

    graph = StateGraph(AgentState) 

 

    graph.add_node("supervisor", supervisor_node) 

    graph.add_node("policy_agent", policy_agent_node) 

    graph.add_node("compliance_agent", compliance_agent_node) 

    graph.add_node("federal_agent", federal_agent_node) 

    graph.add_node("audit_agent", audit_agent_node) 

    graph.add_node("finalize", finalize_node) 

 

    graph.set_entry_point("supervisor") 

 

    def route(state: dict) -> str: 

        selected = state.get("selected_agent", "policy_agent") 

        if selected == "policy_agent": 

            return "policy_agent" 

        if selected == "compliance_agent": 

            return "compliance_agent" 

        if selected == "federal_agent": 

            return "federal_agent" 

        return "audit_agent" 

 

    graph.add_conditional_edges( 

        "supervisor", 

        route, 

        { 

            "policy_agent": "policy_agent", 

            "compliance_agent": "compliance_agent", 

            "federal_agent": "federal_agent", 

            "audit_agent": "audit_agent", 

        }, 

    ) 

 

    graph.add_edge("policy_agent", "finalize") 

    graph.add_edge("compliance_agent", "finalize") 

    graph.add_edge("federal_agent", "finalize") 

    graph.add_edge("audit_agent", "finalize") 

    graph.add_edge("finalize", END) 

 

    return graph.compile()

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

from typing import Literal 

 

from fastapi import Header 

 

 

UserRole = Literal[ 

    "policy_user", 

    "compliance_expert", 

    "federal_officer", 

    "audit", 

] 

 

 

@dataclass(frozen=True) 

class RequestContext: 

    user_id: str 

    user_role: UserRole 

    manager_id: str | None 

    skip_manager_id: str | None 

 

 

def get_request_context( 

    x_user_id: str = Header(default="anonymous", alias="X-User-Id"), 

    x_user_role: str = Header(default="policy_user", alias="X-User-Role"), 

    x_manager_id: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="X-Manager-Id"), 

    x_skip_manager_id: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="X-Skip-Manager-Id"), 

) -> RequestContext: 

    role = (x_user_role or "policy_user").lower().strip() 

    if role not in {"policy_user", "compliance_expert", "federal_officer", "audit"}: 

        role = "policy_user" 

 

    return RequestContext( 

        user_id=(x_user_id or "anonymous").strip(), 

        user_role=role,  # type: ignore[arg-type] 

        manager_id=(x_manager_id or None), 

        skip_manager_id=(x_skip_manager_id or None), 

    ) 

backend/app/security/org_permissions.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

from typing import Literal 

 

 

AccessScope = Literal["self", "team", "org", "audit"] 

 

 

def role_to_scope(role: str) -> AccessScope: 

    role = (role or "policy_user").lower().strip() 

    if role == "policy_user": 

        return "self" 

    if role == "compliance_expert": 

        return "team" 

    if role == "federal_officer": 

        return "org" 

    return "audit" 

 

 

@dataclass(frozen=True) 

class CompareOwnerContext: 

    owner_user_id: str 

    owner_manager_id: str | None = None 

 

 

def can_access_compare( 

    requester_role: str, 

    requester_user_id: str, 

    requester_manager_id: str | None, 

    owner: CompareOwnerContext, 

) -> bool: 

    """ 

    Org-aware authorization: 

    - policy_user        => self only 

    - compliance_expert  => self + same-manager team scope 

    - federal_officer    => org-wide 

    - audit              => full access 

    """ 

    scope = role_to_scope(requester_role) 

 

    if scope == "audit": 

        return True 

 

    if scope == "org": 

        return True 

 

    if scope == "team": 

        if requester_user_id == owner.owner_user_id: 

            return True 

        if requester_manager_id and owner.owner_manager_id: 

            return requester_manager_id == owner.owner_manager_id 

        return False 

 

    return requester_user_id == owner.owner_user_id

backend/app/agents/nodes.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import json 

from typing import Any 

 

import httpx 

from openai import OpenAI 

 

from ..config import Settings 

from .prompts import build_role_prompt 

from .roles import role_to_agent 

from .formatter import format_response_for_role 

 

 

def supervisor_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    role = state.get("user_role", "policy_user") 

    return { 

        "selected_agent": role_to_agent(role), 

    } 

 

 

def policy_agent_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    prompt = build_role_prompt( 

        role="policy_user", 

        query=state.get("query", ""), 

        compare_response=state.get("compare_response", {}), 

        evidence=state.get("evidence", []), 

    ) 

    return {"final_prompt": prompt} 

 

 

def compliance_agent_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    prompt = build_role_prompt( 

        role="compliance_expert", 

        query=state.get("query", ""), 

        compare_response=state.get("compare_response", {}), 

        evidence=state.get("evidence", []), 

    ) 

    return {"final_prompt": prompt} 

 

 

def federal_agent_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    prompt = build_role_prompt( 

        role="federal_officer", 

        query=state.get("query", ""), 

        compare_response=state.get("compare_response", {}), 

        evidence=state.get("evidence", []), 

    ) 

    return {"final_prompt": prompt} 

 

 

def audit_agent_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    prompt = build_role_prompt( 

        role="audit", 

        query=state.get("query", ""), 

        compare_response=state.get("compare_response", {}), 

        evidence=state.get("evidence", []), 

    ) 

    return {"final_prompt": prompt} 

 

 

def _role_response_schema(role: str) -> dict[str, Any]: 

    if role == "policy_user": 

        return { 

            "name": "PolicyUserOverlay", 

            "schema": { 

                "type": "object", 

                "additionalProperties": False, 

                "properties": { 

                    "summary": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 1200}, 

                    "key_changes": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "maxItems": 8}, 

                }, 

                "required": ["summary", "key_changes"], 

            }, 

        } 

 

    if role == "compliance_expert": 

        return { 

            "name": "ComplianceExpertOverlay", 

            "schema": { 

                "type": "object", 

                "additionalProperties": False, 

                "properties": { 

                    "overview": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 1400}, 

                    "risk_notes": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "maxItems": 10}, 

                    "control_notes": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "maxItems": 8}, 

                }, 

                "required": ["overview", "risk_notes", "control_notes"], 

            }, 

        } 

 

    if role == "federal_officer": 

        return { 

            "name": "FederalOfficerOverlay", 

            "schema": { 

                "type": "object", 

                "additionalProperties": False, 

                "properties": { 

                    "summary": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 1400}, 

                    "compliance_findings": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "maxItems": 10}, 

                    "risk_notes": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "maxItems": 10}, 

                }, 

                "required": ["summary", "compliance_findings", "risk_notes"], 

            }, 

        } 

 

    return { 

        "name": "AuditOverlay", 

        "schema": { 

            "type": "object", 

            "additionalProperties": False, 

            "properties": { 

                "summary": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 1400}, 

                "audit_notes": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "maxItems": 10}, 

                "trace_comment": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 600}, 

            }, 

            "required": ["summary", "audit_notes", "trace_comment"], 

        }, 

    } 

 

 

def _call_role_llm(role: str, final_prompt: str, compare_response: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]: 

    settings = Settings() 

 

    if not settings.OPENAI_API_KEY: 

        raise RuntimeError("OPENAI_API_KEY not set") 

 

    base_url = "https://api.openai.com/v1" 

    if not base_url.startswith("https://"): 

        raise RuntimeError("Outbound calls must be HTTPS-only") 

 

    overlay_input = { 

        "role": role, 

        "prompt": final_prompt, 

        "compare_summary": (compare_response or {}).get("summary", {}), 

        "compare_diff": (compare_response or {}).get("diff", {}), 

    } 

 

    user_input = ( 

        "Return ONLY valid JSON that matches the schema. " 

        "Do not invent facts. Use only the supplied prompt and compare payload.\n" 

        + json.dumps(overlay_input, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True) 

    ) 

 

    http_client = httpx.Client( 

        timeout=httpx.Timeout(settings.OUTBOUND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS), 

        verify=True, 

        follow_redirects=False, 

        trust_env=False, 

    ) 

    try: 

        client = OpenAI(api_key=settings.OPENAI_API_KEY, base_url=base_url, http_client=http_client) 

        rsp = client.responses.create( 

            model=settings.OPENAI_MODEL, 

            input=user_input, 

            temperature=0.0, 

            max_output_tokens=min(settings.OPENAI_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS, 500), 

            response_format={"type": "json_schema", "json_schema": _role_response_schema(role)}, 

        ) 

        text = getattr(rsp, "output_text", "") or "{}" 

        return json.loads(text) 

    finally: 

        http_client.close() 

 

 

def _fallback_overlay(role: str, compare_response: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]: 

    summary = (compare_response or {}).get("summary", {}) or {} 

    overview = summary.get("overview", "") 

    key_changes = summary.get("key_changes", []) or [] 

    risk_notes = summary.get("risk_notes", []) or [] 

 

    if role == "policy_user": 

        return { 

            "summary": overview or "Document comparison completed.", 

            "key_changes": key_changes[:8], 

        } 

 

    if role == "compliance_expert": 

        return { 

            "overview": overview or "Detailed compliance comparison completed.", 

            "risk_notes": risk_notes[:10], 

            "control_notes": [], 

        } 

 

    if role == "federal_officer": 

        return { 

            "summary": overview or "Regulatory-focused comparison completed.", 

            "compliance_findings": key_changes[:10], 

            "risk_notes": risk_notes[:10], 

        } 

 

    return { 

        "summary": overview or "Audit comparison completed.", 

        "audit_notes": key_changes[:10], 

        "trace_comment": "Role-based audit overlay generated without external LLM.", 

    } 

 

 

def finalize_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    """ 

    Role-specific LLM call inside finalize_node(): 

    - builds a role-aware overlay using deterministic OpenAI 

    - merges overlay onto the role-specific formatter output 

    - adds audit-only metadata trace block 

    """ 

    role = state.get("user_role", "policy_user") 

    compare_response = state.get("compare_response", {}) or {} 

    final_prompt = state.get("final_prompt", "") 

 

    base = format_response_for_role(compare_response, role) 

 

    try: 

        overlay = _call_role_llm(role, final_prompt, compare_response) 

    except Exception: 

        overlay = _fallback_overlay(role, compare_response) 

 

    if role == "policy_user": 

        base["summary"] = overlay.get("summary", base.get("summary", "")) 

        base["key_changes"] = overlay.get("key_changes", base.get("key_changes", [])) 

 

    elif role == "compliance_expert": 

        base["summary"] = { 

            "overview": overlay.get("overview", ((base.get("summary") or {}).get("overview", ""))), 

            "key_changes": ((base.get("summary") or {}).get("key_changes", [])), 

            "risk_notes": overlay.get("risk_notes", base.get("risk_notes", [])), 

            "control_notes": overlay.get("control_notes", []), 

        } 

        base["risk_notes"] = overlay.get("risk_notes", base.get("risk_notes", [])) 

 

    elif role == "federal_officer": 

        base["summary"] = overlay.get("summary", base.get("summary", "")) 

        base["risk_notes"] = overlay.get("risk_notes", base.get("risk_notes", [])) 

        base["compliance_findings"] = overlay.get("compliance_findings", []) 

 

    else: 

        base["summary"] = overlay.get("summary", ((base.get("summary") or {}).get("overview", ""))) 

        base["audit_notes"] = overlay.get("audit_notes", []) 

        base["trace_comment"] = overlay.get("trace_comment", "") 

        base["metadata"] = { 

            "request_id": compare_response.get("request_id"), 

            "compare_id": compare_response.get("compare_id"), 

            "exact_cache_hit": compare_response.get("exact_cache_hit"), 

            "semantic_cache_hit": compare_response.get("semantic_cache_hit"), 

        } 

 

    return {"final_response": base} 

ui/api_client.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

from typing import Generator, Optional 

 

import httpx 

 

 

@dataclass(frozen=True) 

class SSEEvent: 

    event: str 

    data: str 

 

 

def _headers( 

    api_key: str, 

    request_id: str | None = None, 

    user_role: str | None = None, 

    user_id: str | None = None, 

    manager_id: str | None = None, 

    skip_manager_id: str | None = None, 

) -> dict: 

    h = {"Accept": "application/json"} 

    if api_key: 

        h["X-API-Key"] = api_key 

    if request_id: 

        h["X-Request-Id"] = request_id 

    if user_role: 

        h["X-User-Role"] = user_role 

    if user_id: 

        h["X-User-Id"] = user_id 

    if manager_id: 

        h["X-Manager-Id"] = manager_id 

    if skip_manager_id: 

        h["X-Skip-Manager-Id"] = skip_manager_id 

    return h 

 

 

def compare_role_aware( 

    backend_url: str, 

    api_key: str, 

    file_a_name: str, 

    file_a_bytes: bytes, 

    file_a_type: str, 

    file_b_name: str, 

    file_b_bytes: bytes, 

    file_b_type: str, 

    request_timeout_s: float, 

    user_role: str, 

    user_id: str, 

    manager_id: str | None = None, 

    skip_manager_id: str | None = None, 

    request_id: str | None = None, 

) -> dict: 

    url = f"{backend_url}/compare/role-aware" 

    files = { 

        "file_a": (file_a_name, file_a_bytes, file_a_type or "application/octet-stream"), 

        "file_b": (file_b_name, file_b_bytes, file_b_type or "application/octet-stream"), 

    } 

    headers = _headers( 

        api_key=api_key, 

        request_id=request_id, 

        user_role=user_role, 

        user_id=user_id, 

        manager_id=manager_id, 

        skip_manager_id=skip_manager_id, 

    ) 

 

    with httpx.Client(timeout=httpx.Timeout(request_timeout_s), follow_redirects=False) as client: 

        r = client.post(url, files=files, headers=headers) 

        r.raise_for_status() 

        return r.json() 

 

 

def export_summary( 

    backend_url: str, 

    api_key: str, 

    compare_id: str, 

    request_timeout_s: float, 

    request_id: str | None = None, 

) -> dict: 

    url = f"{backend_url}/export/summary" 

    params = {"compare_id": compare_id} 

    with httpx.Client(timeout=httpx.Timeout(request_timeout_s), follow_redirects=False) as client: 

        r = client.get(url, params=params, headers=_headers(api_key, request_id=request_id)) 

        r.raise_for_status() 

        return r.json() 

 

 

def _iter_sse_lines(resp: httpx.Response) -> Generator[str, None, None]: 

    for line in resp.iter_lines(): 

        if line is None: 

            continue 

        yield line 

 

 

def compare_stream( 

    backend_url: str, 

    api_key: str, 

    file_a_name: str, 

    file_a_bytes: bytes, 

    file_a_type: str, 

    file_b_name: str, 

    file_b_bytes: bytes, 

    file_b_type: str, 

    request_timeout_s: float, 

    user_role: str | None = None, 

    user_id: str | None = None, 

    manager_id: str | None = None, 

    skip_manager_id: str | None = None, 

    request_id: str | None = None, 

) -> Generator[SSEEvent, None, None]: 

    url = f"{backend_url}/compare/stream" 

    files = { 

        "file_a": (file_a_name, file_a_bytes, file_a_type or "application/octet-stream"), 

        "file_b": (file_b_name, file_b_bytes, file_b_type or "application/octet-stream"), 

    } 

    headers = _headers( 

        api_key=api_key, 

        request_id=request_id, 

        user_role=user_role, 

        user_id=user_id, 

        manager_id=manager_id, 

        skip_manager_id=skip_manager_id, 

    ) 

    headers["Accept"] = "text/event-stream" 

 

    with httpx.Client(timeout=httpx.Timeout(request_timeout_s), follow_redirects=False) as client: 

        with client.stream("POST", url, files=files, headers=headers) as resp: 

            resp.raise_for_status() 

            ev_name = "message" 

            data_lines: list[str] = [] 

 

            for raw in _iter_sse_lines(resp): 

                if raw == "": 

                    if data_lines: 

                        yield SSEEvent(event=ev_name, data="\n".join(data_lines)) 

                    ev_name = "message" 

                    data_lines = [] 

                    continue 

 

                if raw.startswith("event:"): 

                    ev_name = raw.split(":", 1)[1].strip() or "message" 

                elif raw.startswith("data:"): 

                    data_lines.append(raw.split(":", 1)[1].lstrip()) 

                else: 

                    continue 

`` 

ui/role_dashboard.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import streamlit as st 

 

 

def _metric_block(label: str, value: str) -> None: 

    st.metric(label, value) 

 

 

def render_role_dashboard(resp: dict, role: str) -> None: 

    role = (role or "policy_user").lower().strip() 

 

    if role == "policy_user": 

        st.subheader("📄 Policy User View") 

        st.write(resp.get("summary", "")) 

        key_changes = resp.get("key_changes", []) or [] 

        if key_changes: 

            st.markdown("### Key Changes") 

            for item in key_changes: 

                st.write(f"- {item}") 

        return 

 

    if role == "compliance_expert": 

        st.subheader("📊 Compliance Expert Dashboard") 

        summary = resp.get("summary", {}) or {} 

        st.write(summary.get("overview", "")) 

        risk_notes = resp.get("risk_notes", []) or [] 

        if risk_notes: 

            st.markdown("### Risk Notes") 

            for risk in risk_notes: 

                st.warning(risk) 

        st.markdown("### Detailed Diff") 

        st.json(resp.get("diff", {}), expanded=False) 

        return 

 

    if role == "federal_officer": 

        st.subheader("🏛 Federal Compliance Officer Dashboard") 

        st.error("Strict evidence-backed compliance mode") 

        st.write(resp.get("summary", "")) 

        findings = resp.get("compliance_findings", []) or [] 

        if findings: 

            st.markdown("### Compliance Findings") 

            for item in findings: 

                st.write(f"- {item}") 

        risk_notes = resp.get("risk_notes", []) or [] 

        if risk_notes: 

            st.markdown("### Risk Notes") 

            for risk in risk_notes: 

                st.error(risk) 

        st.markdown("### Evidence Output") 

        st.json(resp.get("diff", {}), expanded=False) 

        return 

 

    st.subheader("🔍 Audit Team Dashboard") 

    st.write(resp.get("summary", "")) 

 

    metadata = resp.get("metadata", {}) or {} 

    st.markdown("### Trace Metadata") 

    col1, col2, col3, col4 = st.columns(4, gap="small") 

    with col1: 

        _metric_block("Request ID", str(metadata.get("request_id", ""))[:12] or "N/A") 

    with col2: 

        _metric_block("Compare ID", str(metadata.get("compare_id", ""))[:12] or "N/A") 

    with col3: 

        _metric_block("Exact Cache", "Yes" if metadata.get("exact_cache_hit") else "No") 

    with col4: 

        _metric_block("Semantic Cache", "Yes" if metadata.get("semantic_cache_hit") else "No") 

 

    audit_notes = resp.get("audit_notes", []) or [] 

    if audit_notes: 

        st.markdown("### Audit Notes") 

        for note in audit_notes: 

            st.info(note) 

 

    trace_comment = resp.get("trace_comment", "") 

    if trace_comment: 

        st.caption(trace_comment) 

 

    st.markdown("### Full Payload") 

    st.json(resp, expanded=False)

1) Updated backend/app/main.py 

Replace your current compare() route body with the shared helper below, then add the /compare/role-aware route. 
This is written to fit the backend structure we already built earlier. 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import json

import uuid 

from typing import Any, Optional

from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse, StreamingResponse 

from starlette.status import HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND 

 

from . import __version__ 

from .audit import AuditEvent, AuditLogger, now_ts 

from .config import Settings 

from .diff import diff_docx, diff_pdf, diff_tabular, diff_text 

from .models import ( 

    CompareResponse, 

    DocxDiff, 

    DiffPayload, 

    ErrorResponse, 

    ExportSummaryResponse, 

    FeedbackRequest, 

    FeedbackResponse, 

    FileMeta, 

    HealthResponse, 

    LlmSummary, 

    PdfDiff, 

    TabularDiff, 

    TextDiff,

from .parsing import parse_upload

app.state.audit = AuditLogger(log_path="backend/audit.log") 

app.state.rate_limiter = RateLimiter(per_minute=settings.RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE) 

app.state.stores = AppStores(ttl_seconds=settings.CACHE_TTL_SECONDS) 

app.state.exact_cache = ExactCache(ttl_seconds=settings.CACHE_TTL_SECONDS, max_items=settings.MAX_CACHE_ITEMS)

        max_age=600, 

    ) 

 

 

@app.middleware("http") 

async def audit_and_request_id(request: Request, call_next): 

    rid = get_request_id(request) 

    request.state.request_id = rid 

 

    start = time.time() 

    status = 500 

    api_key_present = bool(request.headers.get("X-API-Key") or request.headers.get("Authorization")) 

 

    try: 

        response = await call_next(request) 

        status = response.status_code 

        response.headers["X-Request-Id"] = rid 

        return response 

    finally: 

        latency_ms = int((time.time() - start) * 1000) 

        compare_id = getattr(request.state, "compare_id", None) 

        file_a_sha = getattr(request.state, "file_a_sha256", None) 

        file_b_sha = getattr(request.state, "file_b_sha256", None) 

        exact_hit = getattr(request.state, "exact_cache_hit", None) 

        semantic_hit = getattr(request.state, "semantic_cache_hit", None) 

        notes = "rate_limited" if getattr(request.state, "rate_limited", False) else None 

 

        app.state.audit.write( 

            AuditEvent( 

                ts=now_ts(), 

                request_id=rid, 

                path=str(request.url.path), 

                method=request.method, 

                status=status, 

                latency_ms=latency_ms, 

                api_key_present=api_key_present, 

                file_a_sha256=file_a_sha, 

                file_b_sha256=file_b_sha, 

                compare_id=compare_id, 

                exact_cache_hit=exact_hit, 

                semantic_cache_hit=semantic_hit, 

                notes=notes, 

            ) 

        ) 

 

 

@app.exception_handler(HTTPException) 

async def http_exc_handler(request: Request, exc: HTTPException): 

    rid = getattr(request.state, "request_id", str(uuid.uuid4())) 

    payload = ErrorResponse(request_id=rid, error="http_error", detail=str(exc.detail)) 

    return JSONResponse(status_code=exc.status_code, content=payload.model_dump()) 

 

 

@app.get("/health", response_model=HealthResponse) 

async def health(): 

    return HealthResponse(ok=True, service=settings.APP_NAME, version=__version__) 

 

 

def _file_meta(d) -> FileMeta: 

    return FileMeta( 

        filename=d.filename, 

        content_type=d.content_type, 

        size_bytes=d.size_bytes, 

        sha256=d.sha256, 

        detected_type=d.doc_type, 

    ) 

 

 

def _build_llm_prompt(diff_payload: DiffPayload, file_a: FileMeta, file_b: FileMeta, max_chars: int) -> str: 

    base = { 

        "mode": diff_payload.mode, 

        "file_a": {"name": file_a.filename, "type": file_a.detected_type, "sha256": file_a.sha256}, 

        "file_b": {"name": file_b.filename, "type": file_b.detected_type, "sha256": file_b.sha256}, 

    } 

 

    diff_part: dict[str, Any] = {"summary": {}} 

 

    if diff_payload.mode == "text" and diff_payload.text: 

        d = diff_payload.text 

        diff_part["summary"] = {"added": d.added, "removed": d.removed, "modified": d.modified, "truncated": d.truncated} 

        diff_part["items"] = [i.model_dump() for i in d.items[:80]] 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "docx" and diff_payload.docx: 

        d = diff_payload.docx 

        diff_part["summary"] = {"added": d.added, "removed": d.removed, "modified": d.modified, "truncated": d.truncated} 

        diff_part["items"] = [i.model_dump() for i in d.items[:80]] 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "tabular" and diff_payload.tabular: 

        d = diff_payload.tabular 

        diff_part["schema"] = d.schema.model_dump() 

        diff_part["cell_changes"] = [c.model_dump() for c in d.cell_changes[:120]] 

        diff_part["truncated"] = d.truncated 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "pdf" and diff_payload.pdf: 

        d = diff_payload.pdf 

        diff_part["pages"] = [{"page": p.page, "diff": {"added": p.diff.added, "removed": p.diff.removed, "modified": p.diff.modified}} for p in d.pages[:12]] 

        snippets = [] 

        for p in d.pages[:6]: 

            snippets.extend([i.model_dump() for i in p.diff.items[:10]]) 

        diff_part["items"] = snippets 

        diff_part["truncated"] = d.truncated 

 

    prompt_obj = { 

        "task": "Summarize differences between two documents. Do not guess missing info. Keep concise and business-friendly.", 

        "requirements": { 

            "output_fields": ["status", "overview", "key_changes", "risk_notes", "confidence"], 

            "status_rule": "Use insufficient_evidence if diff content is empty or too truncated to be confident.", 

        }, 

        "context": base, 

        "diff": diff_part, 

    } 

 

    s = json.dumps(prompt_obj, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True) 

    if len(s) > max_chars: 

        s = s[: max_chars - 20] + '"...TRUNCATED"}' 

    return ( 

        "You are a deterministic compliance-grade summarizer.\n" 

        "Return ONLY JSON matching the schema fields:\n" 

        '{"status":"ok|insufficient_evidence","overview":"...","key_changes":["..."],"risk_notes":["..."],"confidence":0.0}\n' 

        "Input:\n" 

        f"{s}" 

    ) 

 

 

def _make_llm(settings: Settings) -> LLMClient: 

    if not settings.OPENAI_API_KEY: 

        raise RuntimeError("OPENAI_API_KEY not set") 

    return LLMClient( 

        api_key=settings.OPENAI_API_KEY, 

        model=settings.OPENAI_MODEL, 

        embed_model=settings.OPENAI_EMBEDDING_MODEL, 

        max_tokens=settings.OPENAI_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS, 

        timeout_s=settings.OUTBOUND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS, 

    ) 

 

 

def _masked(settings: Settings, text: str) -> str: 

    return mask_text(text, config=settings.pii_config(), salt=settings.PII_HASH_SALT) 

 

 

def _compute_diff(settings: Settings, a_doc, b_doc) -> DiffPayload: 

    max_items = settings.MAX_DIFF_ITEMS 

 

    if a_doc.doc_type == "csv" and b_doc.doc_type == "csv": 

        td: TabularDiff = diff_tabular(a_doc.parsed, b_doc.parsed, max_items=max_items) 

        return DiffPayload(mode="tabular", tabular=td) 

 

    if a_doc.doc_type == "xlsx" and b_doc.doc_type == "xlsx": 

        td2: TabularDiff = diff_tabular(a_doc.parsed, b_doc.parsed, max_items=max_items) 

        return DiffPayload(mode="tabular", tabular=td2) 

 

    if a_doc.doc_type == "docx" and b_doc.doc_type == "docx": 

        a_paras = [p.get("text") or "" for p in (a_doc.parsed.get("paragraphs") or [])] 

        b_paras = [p.get("text") or "" for p in (b_doc.parsed.get("paragraphs") or [])] 

        dd: DocxDiff = diff_docx(a_paras, b_paras, max_items=max_items) 

        return DiffPayload(mode="docx", docx=dd) 

 

    if a_doc.doc_type == "pdf" and b_doc.doc_type == "pdf": 

        pd: PdfDiff = diff_pdf(a_doc.parsed.get("pages") or [], b_doc.parsed.get("pages") or [], max_items=max_items) 

        return DiffPayload(mode="pdf", pdf=pd) 

 

    a_text = a_doc.parsed.get("text") or "" 

    b_text = b_doc.parsed.get("text") or "" 

    td3: TextDiff = diff_text(a_text, b_text, max_items=max_items) 

    return DiffPayload(mode="text", text=td3) 

 

 

def _offline_summary(diff_payload: DiffPayload) -> LlmSummary: 

    empty = False 

    if diff_payload.mode == "text": 

        empty = not diff_payload.text or (diff_payload.text.added + diff_payload.text.removed + diff_payload.text.modified == 0) 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "docx": 

        empty = not diff_payload.docx or (diff_payload.docx.added + diff_payload.docx.removed + diff_payload.docx.modified == 0) 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "tabular": 

        empty = not diff_payload.tabular or (len(diff_payload.tabular.cell_changes) == 0 and not diff_payload.tabular.schema.added_columns and not diff_payload.tabular.schema.removed_columns) 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "pdf": 

        empty = not diff_payload.pdf or (len(diff_payload.pdf.pages) == 0) 

 

    status = "insufficient_evidence" if empty else "ok" 

    overview = "OpenAI not configured; returning deterministic summary from diff statistics." 

    if status == "ok": 

        overview = "Differences detected; OpenAI not configured so detailed narrative summary is unavailable." 

    return LlmSummary(status=status, overview=overview, key_changes=[], risk_notes=[], confidence=0.3, cache_hit=False) 

 

 

def _mask_parsed(settings: Settings, parsed: dict) -> dict: 

    if not parsed: 

        return parsed 

    cfg = settings.pii_config() 

    salt = settings.PII_HASH_SALT 

 

    def m(s: str) -> str: 

        return mask_text(s or "", cfg, salt) 

 

    out = dict(parsed) 

 

    if "text" in out and isinstance(out["text"], str): 

        out["text"] = m(out["text"]) 

 

    if "pages" in out and isinstance(out["pages"], list): 

        out["pages"] = [ 

            {**p, "text": m(p.get("text", ""))} if isinstance(p, dict) else p 

            for p in out["pages"] 

        ] 

 

    if "paragraphs" in out and isinstance(out["paragraphs"], list): 

        out["paragraphs"] = [ 

            {**p, "text": m(p.get("text", ""))} if isinstance(p, dict) else p 

            for p in out["paragraphs"] 

        ] 

 

    if "slides" in out and isinstance(out["slides"], list): 

        out["slides"] = [ 

            {**s, "text": m(s.get("text", ""))} if isinstance(s, dict) else s 

            for s in out["slides"] 

        ] 

 

    if "sheets" in out and isinstance(out["sheets"], dict): 

        new_sheets = {} 

        for name, sheet in out["sheets"].items(): 

            if not isinstance(sheet, dict): 

                continue 

            sh2 = dict(sheet) 

            if isinstance(sh2.get("header"), list): 

                sh2["header"] = [m(str(x)) for x in sh2["header"]] 

            if isinstance(sh2.get("rows"), list): 

                sh2["rows"] = [ 

                    [m(str(x)) for x in r] if isinstance(r, list) else r 

                    for r in sh2["rows"] 

                ] 

            new_sheets[str(name)] = sh2 

        out["sheets"] = new_sheets 

 

    return out 

 

 

async def _rate_limit(request: Request, api_key: str): 

    try: 

        request.app.state.rate_limiter.check(request, api_key) 

    except HTTPException: 

        request.state.rate_limited = True 

        raise 

 

 

# NEW: shared core compare pipeline 

async def _run_compare_core( 

    request: Request, 

    file_a: UploadFile, 

    file_b: UploadFile, 

) -> CompareResponse: 

    rid = request.state.request_id 

 

    a_doc = await parse_upload(file_a) 

    b_doc = await parse_upload(file_b) 

 

    a_doc.parsed = _mask_parsed(settings, a_doc.parsed) 

    b_doc.parsed = _mask_parsed(settings, b_doc.parsed) 

 

    request.state.file_a_sha256 = a_doc.sha256 

    request.state.file_b_sha256 = b_doc.sha256 

 

    key_parts = { 

        "a": a_doc.sha256, 

        "b": b_doc.sha256, 

        "types": [a_doc.doc_type, b_doc.doc_type], 

        "max_items": settings.MAX_DIFF_ITEMS, 

        "pii_cfg": settings.pii_config(), 

        "model": settings.OPENAI_MODEL, 

        "embed_model": settings.OPENAI_EMBEDDING_MODEL, 

        "max_tokens": settings.OPENAI_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS, 

        "threshold": settings.SEMANTIC_CACHE_THRESHOLD, 

    } 

    exact_key = stable_compact_hash(key_parts) 

 

    cached = request.app.state.exact_cache.get(exact_key) 

    if cached: 

        resp: CompareResponse = cached 

        resp2 = resp.model_copy(deep=True) 

        resp2.request_id = rid 

        resp2.exact_cache_hit = True 

        request.state.exact_cache_hit = True 

        request.state.semantic_cache_hit = getattr(resp, "semantic_cache_hit", False) 

        request.state.compare_id = resp2.compare_id 

        return resp2 

 

    diff_payload = _compute_diff(settings, a_doc, b_doc) 

    file_meta_a = _file_meta(a_doc) 

    file_meta_b = _file_meta(b_doc) 

    prompt = _build_llm_prompt(diff_payload, file_meta_a, file_meta_b, settings.MAX_TEXT_CHARS_FOR_LLM) 

 

    semantic_hit = False 

    embedding = None 

 

    try: 

        llm = _make_llm(settings) 

        try: 

            emb_key = embedding_cache_key(settings.OPENAI_EMBEDDING_MODEL, prompt) 

            embedding = request.app.state.embedding_cache.get(emb_key) 

            if embedding is None: 

                embedding = llm.embed(prompt) 

                request.app.state.embedding_cache.set(emb_key, embedding) 

 

            semantic_hit, semantic_val = request.app.state.semantic_cache.lookup(embedding) 

            if semantic_hit and semantic_val: 

                resp_prev: CompareResponse = semantic_val 

                resp_prev2 = resp_prev.model_copy(deep=True) 

                resp_prev2.request_id = rid 

                resp_prev2.semantic_cache_hit = True 

                resp_prev2.summary.cache_hit = True 

                request.state.semantic_cache_hit = True 

                request.state.compare_id = resp_prev2.compare_id 

                request.app.state.exact_cache.set(exact_key, resp_prev2.model_copy(deep=True)) 

                request.state.exact_cache_hit = False 

                return resp_prev2 

 

            llm_summary = llm.summarize(prompt) 

        finally: 

            llm.close() 

    except Exception: 

        llm_summary = _offline_summary(diff_payload) 

 

    compare_id = str(uuid.uuid4()) 

    request.state.compare_id = compare_id 

 

    resp = CompareResponse( 

        request_id=rid, 

        compare_id=compare_id, 

        file_a=file_meta_a, 

        file_b=file_meta_b, 

        diff=diff_payload, 

        summary=llm_summary, 

        exact_cache_hit=False, 

        semantic_cache_hit=bool(semantic_hit), 

    ) 

 

    request.app.state.stores.put_compare(compare_id, resp) 

    request.app.state.exact_cache.set(exact_key, resp) 

 

    if embedding and not semantic_hit: 

        hint = stable_compact_hash({"a": a_doc.sha256, "b": b_doc.sha256, "mode": diff_payload.mode, "t": time.time()}) 

        request.app.state.semantic_cache.insert(embedding=embedding, value=resp, key_hint=hint) 

 

    request.state.exact_cache_hit = False 

    request.state.semantic_cache_hit = bool(semantic_hit) 

    return resp 

 

 

@app.post("/compare", response_model=CompareResponse) 

async def compare( 

    request: Request, 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

    file_a: UploadFile = File(...), 

    file_b: UploadFile = File(...), 

): 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

    return await _run_compare_core(request, file_a, file_b) 

 

 

@app.post("/compare/role-aware") 

async def compare_role_aware( 

    request: Request, 

    ctx: RequestContext = Depends(get_request_context), 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

    file_a: UploadFile = File(...), 

    file_b: UploadFile = File(...), 

): 

    """ 

    Reuses existing compare pipeline and applies: 

    1) org-scope authorization 

    2) LangGraph role-agent routing 

    3) role-specific response formatting 

    """ 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

 

    base_resp = await _run_compare_core(request, file_a, file_b) 

 

    # Default: caller owns the compare job unless owner headers are supplied 

    owner_user_id = request.headers.get("X-Owner-User-Id") or ctx.user_id 

    owner_manager_id = request.headers.get("X-Owner-Manager-Id") or ctx.manager_id 

 

    allowed = can_access_compare( 

        requester_role=ctx.user_role, 

        requester_user_id=ctx.user_id, 

        requester_manager_id=ctx.manager_id, 

        owner=CompareOwnerContext( 

            owner_user_id=owner_user_id, 

            owner_manager_id=owner_manager_id, 

        ), 

    ) 

    if not allowed: 

        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not allowed for this org scope") 

 

    compare_dict = base_resp.model_dump() 

 

    evidence = [ 

        { 

            "source": "compare_summary", 

            "text": (compare_dict.get("summary") or {}).get("overview", ""), 

        }, 

        { 

            "source": "compare_diff", 

            "text": json.dumps(compare_dict.get("diff", {}), ensure_ascii=True), 

        }, 

    ] 

 

    state = { 

        "request_id": compare_dict.get("request_id"), 

        "user_id": ctx.user_id, 

        "user_role": ctx.user_role, 

        "manager_id": ctx.manager_id, 

        "skip_manager_id": ctx.skip_manager_id, 

        "access_scope": ( 

            "audit" 

            if ctx.user_role == "audit" 

            else "org" 

            if ctx.user_role == "federal_officer" 

            else "team" 

            if ctx.user_role == "compliance_expert" 

            else "self" 

        ), 

        "query": "Role-aware document comparison", 

        "compare_response": compare_dict, 

        "evidence": evidence, 

    } 

 

    result = ROLE_GRAPH.invoke(state) 

    return result["final_response"] 

 

 

@app.get("/export/summary", response_model=ExportSummaryResponse) 

async def export_summary( 

    request: Request, 

    compare_id: str, 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

): 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

    rid = request.state.request_id 

 

    rec = request.app.state.stores.get_compare(compare_id) 

    if not rec: 

        raise HTTPException(status_code=HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="compare_id not found or expired") 

 

    cfg = settings.pii_config() 

    salt = settings.PII_HASH_SALT 

    s = rec.summary.model_copy(deep=True) 

    s.overview = mask_text(s.overview, cfg, salt) 

    s.key_changes = [mask_text(x, cfg, salt) for x in s.key_changes] 

    s.risk_notes = [mask_text(x, cfg, salt) for x in s.risk_notes] 

 

    return ExportSummaryResponse( 

        request_id=rid, 

        compare_id=compare_id, 

        file_a=rec.file_a, 

        file_b=rec.file_b, 

        summary=s, 

    ) 

 

 

@app.post("/feedback", response_model=FeedbackResponse) 

async def feedback( 

    request: Request, 

    body: FeedbackRequest, 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

): 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

    rid = request.state.request_id 

 

    cfg = settings.pii_config() 

    salt = settings.PII_HASH_SALT 

    body.comment = mask_text(body.comment or "", cfg, salt) 

 

    safe_meta: dict[str, Any] = {} 

    for k, v in (body.metadata or {}).items(): 

        safe_meta[str(k)] = mask_text(v, cfg, salt) if isinstance(v, str) else v 

    body.metadata = safe_meta 

 

    request.app.state.stores.put_feedback(rid, body) 

    return FeedbackResponse(request_id=rid, ok=True)

backend/app/config.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import json 

from typing import Literal 

 

from pydantic import Field 

from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict 

 

 

PIIAction = Literal["redact", "mask", "hash"] 

 

 

class Settings(BaseSettings): 

    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_prefix="", case_sensitive=False) 

 

    APP_NAME: str = "Document Comparison AI Agent (Backend)" 

    LOG_LEVEL: str = "INFO" 

 

    API_KEY: str = Field(default="replace_me_long_random", min_length=12) 

    CORS_ALLOW_ORIGINS: str = Field(default="") 

    RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE: int = Field(default=60, ge=1, le=6000) 

 

    OPENAI_API_KEY: str = Field(default="") 

    OPENAI_MODEL: str = Field(default="gpt-4o-mini") 

    OPENAI_EMBEDDING_MODEL: str = Field(default="text-embedding-3-small") 

    OPENAI_TEMPERATURE: float = Field(default=0.0, ge=0.0, le=0.0) 

    OPENAI_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS: int = Field(default=800, ge=1, le=4096) 

    OUTBOUND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS: float = Field(default=20.0, ge=1.0, le=120.0) 

 

    MAX_DIFF_ITEMS: int = Field(default=400, ge=50, le=5000) 

    MAX_TEXT_CHARS_FOR_LLM: int = Field(default=12000, ge=1000, le=100000) 

 

    CACHE_TTL_SECONDS: int = Field(default=3600, ge=60, le=24 * 3600) 

    SEMANTIC_CACHE_THRESHOLD: float = Field(default=0.92, ge=0.0, le=1.0) 

    MAX_CACHE_ITEMS: int = Field(default=512, ge=64, le=5000) 

 

    PII_HASH_SALT: str = Field(default="change_me_salt", min_length=8) 

    PII_CONFIG_JSON: str = Field( 

        default=json.dumps( 

            { 

                "email": "redact", 

                "phone": "mask", 

                "ssn": "redact", 

                "credit_card": "hash", 

                "ip": "mask", 

            } 

        ) 

    ) 

 

    def cors_origins_list(self) -> list[str]: 

        raw = (self.CORS_ALLOW_ORIGINS or "").strip() 

        if not raw: 

            return [] 

        return [p.strip() for p in raw.split(",") if p.strip()] 

 

    def pii_config(self) -> dict[str, PIIAction]: 

        try: 

            obj = json.loads(self.PII_CONFIG_JSON or "{}") 

            out: dict[str, PIIAction] = {} 

            for k, v in obj.items(): 

                if v in ("redact", "mask", "hash"): 

                    out[str(k).strip().lower()] = v  # type: ignore[assignment] 

            return out or { 

                "email": "redact", 

                "phone": "mask", 

                "ssn": "redact", 

                "credit_card": "hash", 

                "ip": "mask", 

            } 

        except Exception: 

            return { 

                "email": "redact", 

                "phone": "mask", 

                "ssn": "redact", 

                "credit_card": "hash", 

                "ip": "mask", 

            } 

backend/app/models.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from typing import Any, Literal, Optional 

 

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, conint, confloat 

 

 

class HealthResponse(BaseModel): 

    ok: bool = True 

    service: str 

    version: str 

 

 

class FileMeta(BaseModel): 

    filename: str 

    content_type: str 

    size_bytes: conint(ge=0) 

    sha256: str 

    detected_type: Literal["pdf", "txt", "csv", "docx", "xlsx", "pptx"] 

 

 

class ChangeItem(BaseModel): 

    kind: Literal["added", "removed", "modified"] 

    ref: str = Field(description="Reference: line range, paragraph index, page number, cell address, etc.") 

    before: Optional[str] = None 

    after: Optional[str] = None 

 

 

class TextDiff(BaseModel): 

    added: conint(ge=0) 

    removed: conint(ge=0) 

    modified: conint(ge=0) 

    items: list[ChangeItem] 

    truncated: bool = False 

 

 

class TabularSchemaDiff(BaseModel): 

    added_columns: list[str] = [] 

    removed_columns: list[str] = [] 

    common_columns: list[str] = [] 

    added_sheets: list[str] = [] 

    removed_sheets: list[str] = [] 

    common_sheets: list[str] = [] 

 

 

class TabularCellChange(BaseModel): 

    sheet: str 

    cell: str 

    before: Optional[str] = None 

    after: Optional[str] = None 

 

 

class TabularDiff(BaseModel): 

    schema: TabularSchemaDiff 

    cell_changes: list[TabularCellChange] = [] 

    truncated: bool = False 

 

 

class DocxDiff(BaseModel): 

    added: conint(ge=0) 

    removed: conint(ge=0) 

    modified: conint(ge=0) 

    items: list[ChangeItem] 

    truncated: bool = False 

 

 

class PdfPageDiff(BaseModel): 

    page: conint(ge=1) 

    diff: TextDiff 

 

 

class PdfDiff(BaseModel): 

    pages: list[PdfPageDiff] 

    truncated: bool = False 

 

 

class DiffPayload(BaseModel): 

    mode: Literal["text", "tabular", "docx", "pdf"] 

    text: Optional[TextDiff] = None 

    tabular: Optional[TabularDiff] = None 

    docx: Optional[DocxDiff] = None 

    pdf: Optional[PdfDiff] = None 

 

 

class LlmSummary(BaseModel): 

    status: Literal["ok", "insufficient_evidence"] 

    overview: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000) 

    key_changes: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=20) 

    risk_notes: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=20) 

    confidence: confloat(ge=0.0, le=1.0) = 0.7 

    cache_hit: bool = False 

 

 

class CompareResponse(BaseModel): 

    request_id: str 

    compare_id: str 

    file_a: FileMeta 

    file_b: FileMeta 

    diff: DiffPayload 

    summary: LlmSummary 

    exact_cache_hit: bool = False 

    semantic_cache_hit: bool = False 

 

 

class ExportSummaryResponse(BaseModel): 

    request_id: str 

    compare_id: str 

    file_a: FileMeta 

    file_b: FileMeta 

    summary: LlmSummary 

 

 

class FeedbackRequest(BaseModel): 

    compare_id: str = Field(min_length=8) 

    rating: conint(ge=1, le=5) 

    comment: str = Field(default="", max_length=2000) 

    metadata: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict) 

 

 

class FeedbackResponse(BaseModel): 

    request_id: str 

    ok: bool = True 

 

 

class ErrorResponse(BaseModel): 

    request_id: str 

    error: str 

    detail: str 

backend/app/rate_limit.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import time 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

from typing import Dict 

 

from fastapi import HTTPException, Request 

from starlette.status import HTTP_429_TOO_MANY_REQUESTS 

 

 

@dataclass 

class Bucket: 

    window_start: float 

    count: int 

 

 

class RateLimiter: 

    def __init__(self, per_minute: int): 

        self.per_minute = per_minute 

        self._buckets: Dict[str, Bucket] = {} 

 

    def _key(self, request: Request, api_key: str) -> str: 

        ip = request.client.host if request.client else "unknown" 

        return f"{api_key}:{ip}" 

 

    def check(self, request: Request, api_key: str) -> None: 

        now = time.time() 

        key = self._key(request, api_key) 

        b = self._buckets.get(key) 

        if b is None: 

            self._buckets[key] = Bucket(window_start=now, count=1) 

            return 

        if now - b.window_start >= 60.0: 

            b.window_start = now 

            b.count = 1 

            return 

        b.count += 1 

        if b.count > self.per_minute: 

            raise HTTPException(status_code=HTTP_429_TOO_MANY_REQUESTS, detail="Rate limit exceeded")

backend/app/audit.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import json 

import os 

import time 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

from typing import Any, Optional 

 

 

@dataclass 

class AuditEvent: 

    ts: float 

    request_id: str 

    path: str 

    method: str 

    status: int 

    latency_ms: int 

    api_key_present: bool 

    file_a_sha256: Optional[str] = None 

    file_b_sha256: Optional[str] = None 

    compare_id: Optional[str] = None 

    exact_cache_hit: Optional[bool] = None 

    semantic_cache_hit: Optional[bool] = None 

    notes: Optional[str] = None 

 

 

class AuditLogger: 

    def __init__(self, log_path: str): 

        self.log_path = log_path 

        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(log_path) or ".", exist_ok=True) 

 

    def write(self, event: AuditEvent) -> None: 

        payload: dict[str, Any] = { 

            "ts": event.ts, 

            "request_id": event.request_id, 

            "path": event.path, 

            "method": event.method, 

            "status": event.status, 

            "latency_ms": event.latency_ms, 

            "api_key_present": event.api_key_present, 

        } 

        for k in ("file_a_sha256", "file_b_sha256", "compare_id", "exact_cache_hit", "semantic_cache_hit", "notes"): 

            v = getattr(event, k) 

            if v is not None: 

                payload[k] = v 

 

        line = json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True) 

        with open(self.log_path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f: 

            f.write(line + "\n") 

 

 

def now_ts() -> float: 

    return time.time()

backend/app/pii.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import hashlib 

import re 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

from typing import Callable, Literal 

 

PIIAction = Literal["redact", "mask", "hash"] 

 

 

@dataclass(frozen=True) 

class PIIPattern: 

    name: str 

    regex: re.Pattern 

    masker: Callable[[str], str] 

 

 

_EMAIL_RE = re.compile(r"\b[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}\b", re.IGNORECASE) 

_PHONE_RE = re.compile(r"\b(?:\+?\d{1,3}[-.\s]?)?(?:\(?\d{3}\)?[-.\s]?)?\d{3}[-.\s]?\d{4}\b") 

_SSN_RE = re.compile(r"\b\d{3}-\d{2}-\d{4}\b") 

_CC_RE = re.compile(r"\b(?:\d[ -]*?){13,19}\b") 

_IP_RE = re.compile(r"\b(?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}\b") 

 

 

def _mask_keep_last(s: str, last: int = 4) -> str: 

    s2 = re.sub(r"\s+", "", s) 

    if len(s2) <= last: 

        return "*" * len(s2) 

    return "*" * (len(s2) - last) + s2[-last:] 

 

 

def _mask_email(s: str) -> str: 

    try: 

        user, dom = s.split("@", 1) 

        return f"{(user[:1] + '***') if user else '***'}@{dom}" 

    except Exception: 

        return "***@***" 

 

 

def _mask_ip(s: str) -> str: 

    parts = s.split(".") 

    if len(parts) == 4: 

        return ".".join([parts[0], "***", "***", parts[3]]) 

    return "***.***.***.***" 

 

 

PATTERNS: list[PIIPattern] = [ 

    PIIPattern("email", _EMAIL_RE, _mask_email), 

    PIIPattern("phone", _PHONE_RE, lambda s: _mask_keep_last(s, 4)), 

    PIIPattern("ssn", _SSN_RE, lambda s: "***-**-" + _mask_keep_last(s, 4)), 

    PIIPattern("credit_card", _CC_RE, lambda s: _mask_keep_last(s, 4)), 

    PIIPattern("ip", _IP_RE, _mask_ip), 

] 

 

 

def _hash_value(value: str, salt: str) -> str: 

    return hashlib.sha256((salt + value).encode("utf-8", errors="ignore")).hexdigest() 

 

 

def mask_text(text: str, config: dict[str, PIIAction], salt: str) -> str: 

    if not text: 

        return text 

 

    out = text 

    for p in PATTERNS: 

        action = config.get(p.name, "redact") 

        if action == "redact": 

            out = p.regex.sub(f"[REDACTED:{p.name.upper()}]", out) 

        elif action == "mask": 

            out = p.regex.sub(lambda m: f"[MASKED:{p.name.upper()}:{p.masker(m.group(0))}]", out) 

        elif action == "hash": 

            out = p.regex.sub(lambda m: f"[HASHED:{p.name.upper()}:{_hash_value(m.group(0), salt)[:12]}]", out) 

        else: 

            out = p.regex.sub(f"[REDACTED:{p.name.upper()}]", out) 

    return out 

`` 

backend/app/hashing.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import hashlib 

import json 

from typing import Any 

 

 

def sha256_bytes(data: bytes) -> str: 

    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() 

 

 

def sha256_text(text: str) -> str: 

    return hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8", errors="ignore")).hexdigest() 

 

 

def stable_compact_hash(obj: Any) -> str: 

    payload = json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True) 

    return sha256_text(payload)

from fastapi import Header 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

 

 

@dataclass 

class RequestContext: 

    user_id: str 

    user_role: str 

    manager_id: str | None 

    skip_manager_id: str | None 

 

 

def get_request_context( 

    x_user_id: str = Header("anonymous", alias="X-User-Id"), 

    x_user_role: str = Header("policy_user", alias="X-User-Role"), 

    x_manager_id: str | None = Header(None, alias="X-Manager-Id"), 

    x_skip_manager_id: str | None = Header(None, alias="X-Skip-Manager-Id"), 

) -> RequestContext: 

    return RequestContext( 

        user_id=x_user_id, 

        user_role=x_user_role.lower(), 

        manager_id=x_manager_id, 

        skip_manager_id=x_skip_manager_id, 

    ) 

✅ 2. backend/app/security/org_permissions.py 

def can_access_compare(role, requester_id, requester_manager, owner_id, owner_manager): 

    if role == "audit": 

        return True 

    if role == "federal_officer": 

        return True 

    if role == "compliance_expert": 

        return requester_id == owner_id or requester_manager == owner_manager 

    return requester_id == owner_id 

✅ 3. backend/app/agents/roles.py 

def role_to_agent(role: str) -> str: 

    return { 

        "policy_user": "policy_agent", 

        "compliance_expert": "compliance_agent", 

        "federal_officer": "federal_agent", 

        "audit": "audit_agent", 

    }.get(role, "policy_agent") 

✅ 4. backend/app/agents/prompts.py 

def build_prompt(role: str, query: str, compare_response: dict) -> str: 

    if role == "policy_user": 

        instruction = "Explain simply." 

    elif role == "compliance_expert": 

        instruction = "Provide detailed risk analysis." 

    elif role == "federal_officer": 

        instruction = "Strict compliance mode. Evidence only." 

    else: 

        instruction = "Full audit trace and reasoning." 

 

    return f""" 

    ROLE: {role} 

    INSTRUCTION: {instruction} 

    QUERY: {query} 

    DATA: {compare_response} 

    """

        )

✅ 7. backend/app/agents/graph.py 

from langgraph.graph import StateGraph, END 

from .nodes import supervisor, agent_node, finalize 

 

 

def build_graph(): 

 

    g = StateGraph(dict) 

 

    g.add_node("supervisor", supervisor) 

    g.add_node("agent", agent_node) 

    g.add_node("finalize", finalize) 

 

    g.set_entry_point("supervisor") 

 

    g.add_edge("supervisor", "agent") 

    g.add_edge("agent", "finalize") 

    g.add_edge("finalize", END) 

 

    return g.compile()

__all__ = ["LangSmithTracer"] 

backend/app/observability/langsmith_tracer.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import os 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

from typing import Any, Optional 

 

 

@dataclass 

class TraceHandle: 

    run_id: str | None 

 

 

class LangSmithTracer: 

    """ 

    Soft dependency wrapper. 

    If LangSmith env/config is absent, methods still work and return None run_id safely. 

    """ 

 

    def __init__(self) -> None: 

        self.enabled = os.getenv("LANGCHAIN_TRACING_V2", "false").lower() == "true" 

        self.project = os.getenv("LANGCHAIN_PROJECT", "doc-compare-agent") 

        self.api_key = os.getenv("LANGCHAIN_API_KEY", "") 

        self._client = None 

 

        if self.enabled and self.api_key: 

            try: 

                from langsmith import Client  # type: ignore 

                self._client = Client(api_key=self.api_key) 

            except Exception: 

                self._client = None 

                self.enabled = False 

 

    def start_trace(self, name: str, inputs: dict[str, Any] | None = None, metadata: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> TraceHandle: 

        if not self.enabled or self._client is None: 

            return TraceHandle(run_id=None) 

        try: 

            run = self._client.create_run( 

                name=name, 

                run_type="chain", 

                project_name=self.project, 

                inputs=inputs or {}, 

                extra={"metadata": metadata or {}}, 

            ) 

            return TraceHandle(run_id=str(getattr(run, "id", None) or "")) 

        except Exception: 

            return TraceHandle(run_id=None) 

 

    def end_trace(self, handle: TraceHandle, outputs: dict[str, Any] | None = None, error: str | None = None) -> None: 

        if not self.enabled or self._client is None or not handle.run_id: 

            return 

        try: 

            if error: 

                self._client.update_run(run_id=handle.run_id, error=error, outputs=outputs or {}) 

            else: 

                self._client.update_run(run_id=handle.run_id, outputs=outputs or {}) 

        except Exception: 

            return 

 

    def create_feedback(self, run_id: str | None, rating: int, comment: str = "", metadata: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> None: 

        if not self.enabled or self._client is None or not run_id: 

            return 

        try: 

            self._client.create_feedback( 

                run_id=run_id, 

                key="user_feedback", 

                score=float(rating), 

                comment=comment, 

                source_info=metadata or {}, 

            ) 

        except Exception: 

            return
