backend/app/security/jwt_auth.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import json 

import os 

from typing import Any 

 

from fastapi import Header, HTTPException, Request 

from jose import jwt, jwk 

from jose.utils import base64url_decode 

 

_AZURE_OPENID_CONFIG = "https://login.microsoftonline.com/{tenant}/v2.0/.well-known/openid-configuration" 

 

 

class AuthPrincipal(dict): 

    """ 

    { 

      "sub": "...", 

      "name": "...", 

      "email": "...", 

      "roles": [...], 

      "auth_type": "api_key" | "jwt" 

    } 

    """ 

 

 

def _extract_api_key(x_api_key: str | None, authorization: str | None) -> str | None: 

    if x_api_key: 

        return x_api_key.strip() 

    if authorization and authorization.lower().startswith("bearer "): 

        return authorization[7:].strip() 

    return None 

 

 

def _validate_api_key(request: Request, x_api_key: str | None, authorization: str | None) -> AuthPrincipal | None: 

    settings = request.app.state.settings 

    key = _extract_api_key(x_api_key, authorization) 

    if key and key == settings.API_KEY: 

        return AuthPrincipal( 

            sub="api-key-user", 

            name="API Key User", 

            email="", 

            roles=["audit", "federal_officer", "compliance_expert", "policy_user"], 

            auth_type="api_key", 

        ) 

    return None 

 

 

def _fetch_jwks(tenant_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]: 

    import httpx 

 

    oidc = _AZURE_OPENID_CONFIG.format(tenant=tenant_id) 

    with httpx.Client(timeout=10.0, verify=True, follow_redirects=False, trust_env=False) as client: 

        oidc_resp = client.get(oidc) 

        oidc_resp.raise_for_status() 

        openid = oidc_resp.json() 

        jwks_uri = openid["jwks_uri"] 

        jwks_resp = client.get(jwks_uri) 

        jwks_resp.raise_for_status() 

        return jwks_resp.json() 

 

 

def _validate_jwt(token: str) -> AuthPrincipal: 

    tenant_id = os.getenv("AZURE_TENANT_ID", "").strip() 

    audience = os.getenv("AZURE_CLIENT_ID", "").strip() 

    issuer_v2 = f"https://login.microsoftonline.com/{tenant_id}/v2.0" 

 

    if not tenant_id or not audience: 

        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="JWT auth not configured") 

 

    try: 

        unverified_header = jwt.get_unverified_header(token) 

        kid = unverified_header["kid"] 

        jwks = _fetch_jwks(tenant_id) 

 

        key = next((k for k in jwks["keys"] if k["kid"] == kid), None) 

        if not key: 

            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid token key") 

 

        public_key = jwk.construct(key) 

        message, encoded_sig = str(token).rsplit(".", 1) 

        decoded_sig = base64url_decode(encoded_sig.encode("utf-8")) 

 

        if not public_key.verify(message.encode("utf-8"), decoded_sig): 

            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid token signature") 

 

        claims = jwt.get_unverified_claims(token) 

 

        if claims.get("aud") != audience: 

            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid token audience") 

 

        iss = claims.get("iss", "") 

        if iss not in {issuer_v2, f"https://sts.windows.net/{tenant_id}/"}: 

            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid token issuer") 

 

        roles = claims.get("roles", []) or claims.get("groups", []) or [] 

        email = claims.get("preferred_username", "") or claims.get("upn", "") 

        name = claims.get("name", "") or email 

        sub = claims.get("oid", "") or claims.get("sub", "") 

 

        return AuthPrincipal( 

            sub=sub, 

            name=name, 

            email=email, 

            roles=roles if isinstance(roles, list) else [str(roles)], 

            auth_type="jwt", 

        ) 

    except HTTPException: 

        raise 

    except Exception: 

        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="JWT validation failed") 

 

 

async def require_bearer_or_api_key( 

    request: Request, 

    x_api_key: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="X-API-Key"), 

    authorization: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="Authorization"), 

) -> AuthPrincipal: 

    # 1) API key fallback (useful for local dev / service-to-service) 

    principal = _validate_api_key(request, x_api_key, authorization) 

    if principal: 

        return principal 

 

    # 2) JWT bearer auth 

    if not authorization or not authorization.lower().startswith("bearer "): 

        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Missing bearer token or valid API key") 

 

    token = authorization[7:].strip() 

    return _validate_jwt(token) 

backend/app/security/context.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from dataclasses import dataclass 

from fastapi import Header 

 

from .jwt_auth import AuthPrincipal 

 

 

@dataclass(frozen=True) 

class RequestContext: 

    user_id: str 

    user_role: str 

    manager_id: str | None 

    skip_manager_id: str | None 

 

 

def _normalize_role(role: str) -> str: 

    role = (role or "").lower().strip() 

    if role in {"policy_user", "compliance_expert", "federal_officer", "audit"}: 

        return role 

    return "policy_user" 

 

 

def get_request_context( 

    principal: AuthPrincipal, 

    x_user_role: str = Header(default="policy_user", alias="X-User-Role"), 

    x_manager_id: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="X-Manager-Id"), 

    x_skip_manager_id: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="X-Skip-Manager-Id"), 

) -> RequestContext: 

    """ 

    Role is selected by the caller/UI, but only within the role list already granted in JWT/API principal. 

    """ 

    requested = _normalize_role(x_user_role) 

    granted_roles = set([str(r).lower() for r in principal.get("roles", [])]) 

 

    # For API key users, allow any requested role. 

    if principal.get("auth_type") != "api_key" and granted_roles: 

        allowed_map = { 

            "policy_user": {"policy_user", "employee"}, 

            "compliance_expert": {"compliance_expert", "compliance"}, 

            "federal_officer": {"federal_officer", "regulatory", "regulator"}, 

            "audit": {"audit", "auditor"}, 

        } 

        accepted = any(role in granted_roles for role in allowed_map.get(requested, {requested})) 

        effective_role = requested if accepted else "policy_user" 

    else: 

        effective_role = requested 

 

    return RequestContext( 

        user_id=(principal.get("sub") or principal.get("email") or "anonymous"), 

        user_role=effective_role, 

        manager_id=x_manager_id, 

        skip_manager_id=x_skip_manager_id, 

    )

backend/app/auth_jwt.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import os 

import time 

from typing import Optional, Any 

 

from fastapi import Depends, Header, HTTPException, Request 

from jose import jwt, JWTError 

 

# Enterprise-grade auth: 

# - Supports local JWT validation 

# - Ready for Azure AD / IAM by validating issuer & audience 

# - Keeps role and org claims in request context 

 

JWT_ALG = os.getenv("JWT_ALG", "RS256") 

JWT_ISSUER = os.getenv("JWT_ISSUER", "https://login.microsoftonline.com/common/v2.0") 

JWT_AUDIENCE = os.getenv("JWT_AUDIENCE", "api://doc-compare-agent") 

JWT_PUBLIC_KEY = os.getenv("JWT_PUBLIC_KEY", "")  # RS256 public key / PEM for local validation 

 

 

class AuthContext(dict): 

    """ 

    Dict-like auth context: 

      sub 

      role 

      manager_id 

      skip_manager_id 

      groups 

      raw_claims 

    """ 

    pass 

 

 

def _extract_bearer(auth_header: str | None) -> str | None: 

    if not auth_header: 

        return None 

    val = auth_header.strip() 

    if val.lower().startswith("bearer "): 

        return val[7:].strip() 

    return None 

 

 

def decode_jwt_token(token: str) -> dict[str, Any]: 

    if not JWT_PUBLIC_KEY: 

        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="JWT_PUBLIC_KEY not configured") 

 

    try: 

        claims = jwt.decode( 

            token, 

            JWT_PUBLIC_KEY, 

            algorithms=[JWT_ALG], 

            audience=JWT_AUDIENCE, 

            issuer=JWT_ISSUER, 

            options={"verify_at_hash": False}, 

        ) 

        return claims 

    except JWTError: 

        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid JWT token") 

 

 

def _normalize_role(claims: dict[str, Any]) -> str: 

    role = ( 

        claims.get("role") 

        or claims.get("roles") 

        or claims.get("app_role") 

        or "policy_user" 

    ) 

    if isinstance(role, list): 

        role = role[0] if role else "policy_user" 

    role = str(role).lower().strip() 

 

    allowed = {"policy_user", "compliance_expert", "federal_officer", "audit"} 

    return role if role in allowed else "policy_user" 

 

 

async def get_auth_context( 

    request: Request, 

    authorization: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="Authorization"), 

    x_user_role: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="X-User-Role"), 

    x_user_id: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="X-User-Id"), 

    x_manager_id: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="X-Manager-Id"), 

    x_skip_manager_id: str | None = Header(default=None, alias="X-Skip-Manager-Id"), 

) -> AuthContext: 

    """ 

    Priority: 

    1) JWT bearer token (enterprise) 

    2) explicit headers fallback (dev/demo) 

    """ 

    token = _extract_bearer(authorization) 

    if token: 

        claims = decode_jwt_token(token) 

        ctx = AuthContext( 

            sub=str(claims.get("sub") or claims.get("oid") or claims.get("upn") or "unknown"), 

            role=_normalize_role(claims), 

            manager_id=claims.get("manager_id"), 

            skip_manager_id=claims.get("skip_manager_id"), 

            groups=claims.get("groups", []), 

            raw_claims=claims, 

        ) 

        request.state.auth_sub = ctx["sub"] 

        request.state.auth_role = ctx["role"] 

        return ctx 

 

    # Dev/demo fallback 

    ctx = AuthContext( 

        sub=(x_user_id or "anonymous").strip(), 

        role=(x_user_role or "policy_user").lower().strip(), 

        manager_id=x_manager_id, 

        skip_manager_id=x_skip_manager_id, 

        groups=[], 

        raw_claims={}, 

    ) 

    request.state.auth_sub = ctx["sub"] 

    request.state.auth_role = ctx["role"] 

    return ctx 

backend/app/observability/langsmith_tracing.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import os 

from typing import Any, Callable 

 

LANGCHAIN_TRACING_V2 = os.getenv("LANGCHAIN_TRACING_V2", "false").lower() == "true" 

LANGCHAIN_PROJECT = os.getenv("LANGCHAIN_PROJECT", "doc-compare-agent") 

LANGCHAIN_API_KEY = os.getenv("LANGCHAIN_API_KEY", "") 

 

# Safe import pattern 

try: 

    from langsmith import traceable, Client 

except Exception: 

    traceable = None 

    Client = None 

 

 

def tracing_enabled() -> bool: 

    return bool(LANGCHAIN_TRACING_V2 and LANGCHAIN_API_KEY and traceable is not None) 

 

 

def get_client(): 

    if Client is None or not LANGCHAIN_API_KEY: 

        return None 

    return Client(api_key=LANGCHAIN_API_KEY) 

 

 

def traced(name: str) -> Callable: 

    """ 

    Decorator factory: 

    - If LangSmith available, wraps function with traceable(name=...) 

    - Else returns original function unchanged 

    """ 

    def decorator(fn: Callable) -> Callable: 

        if tracing_enabled(): 

            return traceable(name=name)(fn) 

        return fn 

    return decorator 

 

 

def create_feedback(run_id: str, rating: int, comment: str, correction: str | None = None) -> None: 

    client = get_client() 

    if client is None or not run_id: 

        return 

    try: 

        client.create_feedback( 

            run_id=run_id, 

            key="user_rating", 

            score=float(rating), 

            comment=comment, 

            correction={"text": correction} if correction else None, 

        ) 

    except Exception: 

        # non-fatal 

        return 

backend/app/eval/role_eval.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import os 

from typing import Any 

 

DEEPEVAL_ENABLED = os.getenv("DEEPEVAL_ENABLED", "false").lower() == "true" 

 

try: 

    from deepeval.metrics import FaithfulnessMetric, AnswerRelevancyMetric 

    from deepeval.test_case import LLMTestCase 

    from deepeval import evaluate 

except Exception: 

    FaithfulnessMetric = None 

    AnswerRelevancyMetric = None 

    LLMTestCase = None 

    evaluate = None 

 

 

ROLE_THRESHOLDS = { 

    "policy_user": {"faithfulness": 0.80, "answer_relevancy": 0.78}, 

    "compliance_expert": {"faithfulness": 0.85, "answer_relevancy": 0.80}, 

    "federal_officer": {"faithfulness": 0.90, "answer_relevancy": 0.82}, 

    "audit": {"faithfulness": 0.88, "answer_relevancy": 0.80}, 

} 

 

 

def eval_role_output( 

    role: str, 

    prompt: str, 

    actual_output: str, 

    expected_output: str | None = None, 

) -> dict[str, Any]: 

    if not DEEPEVAL_ENABLED or evaluate is None or LLMTestCase is None: 

        return { 

            "enabled": False, 

            "passed": True, 

            "faithfulness": None, 

            "answer_relevancy": None, 

        } 

 

    t = ROLE_THRESHOLDS.get(role, ROLE_THRESHOLDS["policy_user"]) 

 

    case = LLMTestCase( 

        input=prompt, 

        actual_output=actual_output, 

        expected_output=expected_output or actual_output, 

    ) 

 

    faith = FaithfulnessMetric(threshold=t["faithfulness"]) 

    rel = AnswerRelevancyMetric(threshold=t["answer_relevancy"]) 

 

    try: 

        results = evaluate(test_cases=[case], metrics=[faith, rel]) 

        f_score = getattr(faith, "score", None) 

        r_score = getattr(rel, "score", None) 

        passed = True 

        if f_score is not None and f_score < t["faithfulness"]: 

            passed = False 

        if r_score is not None and r_score < t["answer_relevancy"]: 

            passed = False 

 

        return { 

            "enabled": True, 

            "passed": passed, 

            "faithfulness": f_score, 

            "answer_relevancy": r_score, 

        } 

    except Exception: 

        return { 

            "enabled": True, 

            "passed": True, 

            "faithfulness": None, 

            "answer_relevancy": None, 

        } 

backend/app/agents/state_enterprise.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from typing import Any, Literal, TypedDict 

 

 

UserRole = Literal[ 

    "policy_user", 

    "compliance_expert", 

    "federal_officer", 

    "audit", 

] 

 

AgentName = Literal[ 

    "router", 

    "policy_agent", 

    "compliance_agent", 

    "federal_agent", 

    "audit_agent", 

    "validator", 

    "critic", 

] 

 

AccessScope = Literal["self", "team", "org", "audit"] 

 

 

class EnterpriseAgentState(TypedDict, total=False): 

    request_id: str 

    user_id: str

backend/app/agents/nodes_enterprise.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import json 

from typing import Any 

 

import_outputimport httpx 

from .prompts import build_role_prompt 

from .formatter import format_response_for_role 

 

 

def _schema_for_role(role: str) -> dict[str, Any]: 

    if role == "policy_user": 

        return { 

            "name": "PolicyOverlay", 

            "schema": { 

                "type": "object", 

                "additionalProperties": False, 

                "properties": { 

                    "summary": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 1200}, 

                    "key_changes": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "maxItems": 8}, 

                }, 

                "required": ["summary", "key_changes"], 

            }, 

        } 

 

    if role == "compliance_expert": 

        return { 

            "name": "ComplianceOverlay", 

            "schema": { 

                "type": "object", 

                "additionalProperties": False, 

                "properties": { 

                    "overview": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 1400}, 

                    "risk_notes": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "maxItems": 10}, 

                    "control_notes": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "maxItems": 8}, 

                }, 

                "required": ["overview", "risk_notes", "control_notes"], 

            }, 

        } 

 

    if role == "federal_officer": 

        return { 

            "name": "FederalOverlay", 

            "schema": { 

                "type": "object", 

                "additionalProperties": False, 

                "properties": { 

                    "summary": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 1400}, 

                    "compliance_findings": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "maxItems": 10}, 

                    "risk_notes": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "maxItems": 10}, 

                }, 

                "required": ["summary", "compliance_findings", "risk_notes"], 

            }, 

        } 

 

    return { 

        "name": "AuditOverlay", 

        "schema": { 

            "type": "object", 

            "additionalProperties": False, 

            "properties": { 

                "summary": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 1400}, 

                "audit_notes": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "maxItems": 10}, 

                "trace_comment": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 600}, 

            }, 

            "required": ["summary", "audit_notes", "trace_comment"], 

        }, 

    } 

 

 

def _llm_overlay(role: str, final_prompt: str, compare_response: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], str | None]: 

    settings = Settings() 

 

    if not settings.OPENAI_API_KEY: 

        raise RuntimeError("OPENAI_API_KEY not set") 

 

    base_url = "https://api.openai.com/v1" 

    if not base_url.startswith("https://"): 

        raise RuntimeError("Outbound calls must be HTTPS-only") 

 

    user_input = json.dumps( 

        { 

            "role": role, 

            "prompt": final_prompt, 

            "compare_summary": (compare_response or {}).get("summary", {}), 

            "compare_diff": (compare_response or {}).get("diff", {}), 

        }, 

        separators=(",", ":"), 

        ensure_ascii=True, 

    ) 

 

    http_client = httpx.Client( 

        timeout=httpx.Timeout(settings.OUTBOUND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS), 

        verify=True, 

        follow_redirects=False, 

        trust_env=False, 

    ) 

    try: 

        client = OpenAI(api_key=settings.OPENAI_API_KEY, base_url=base_url, http_client=http_client) 

 

        rsp = client.responses.create( 

            model=settings.OPENAI_MODEL, 

            input=( 

                "Return ONLY valid JSON matching the schema. " 

                "Use only the provided compare payload. Do not invent facts.\n" + user_input 

            ), 

            temperature=0.0, 

            max_output_tokens=min(settings.OPENAI_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS, 500), 

            response_format={"type": "json_schema", "json_schema": _schema_for_role(role)}, 

        ) 

 

        text = getattr(rsp, "output_text", "") or "{}" 

        run_id = getattr(rsp, "id", None) 

        return json.loads(text), run_id 

    finally: 

        http_client.close() 

 

 

def _fallback_overlay(role: str, compare_response: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]: 

    summary = (compare_response or {}).get("summary", {}) or {} 

    overview = summary.get("overview", "") 

    key_changes = summary.get("key_changes", []) or [] 

    risk_notes = summary.get("risk_notes", []) or [] 

 

    if role == "policy_user": 

        return {"summary": overview or "Comparison completed.", "key_changes": key_changes[:8]} 

 

    if role == "compliance_expert": 

        return { 

            "overview": overview or "Detailed compliance comparison completed.", 

            "risk_notes": risk_notes[:10], 

            "control_notes": [], 

        } 

 

    if role == "federal_officer": 

        return { 

            "summary": overview or "Regulatory-focused comparison completed.", 

            "compliance_findings": key_changes[:10], 

            "risk_notes": risk_notes[:10], 

        } 

 

    return { 

        "summary": overview or "Audit comparison completed.", 

        "audit_notes": key_changes[:10], 

        "trace_comment": "Fallback audit overlay generated without LLM.", 

    } 

 

 

@traced(name="role_router") 

def router_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    # selected_agent should already be set by supervisor/router graph edge, 

    # but we keep this as a traced explicit node for observability. 

    return {"selected_agent": state.get("selected_agent")} 

 

 

@traced(name="role_prompt_builder") 

def specialist_prompt_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    role = state.get("user_role", "policy_user") 

    prompt = build_role_prompt( 

        role=role, 

        query=state.get("query", ""), 

        compare_response=state.get("compare_response", {}), 

        evidence=state.get("evidence", []), 

    ) 

    return {"final_prompt": prompt} 

 

 

@traced(name="role_overlay_llm") 

def role_overlay_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    role = state.get("user_role", "policy_user") 

    compare_response = state.get("compare_response", {}) 

    final_prompt = state.get("final_prompt", "") 

 

    try: 

        overlay, run_id = _llm_overlay(role, final_prompt, compare_response) 

    except Exception: 

        overlay = _fallback_overlay(role, compare_response) 

        run_id = None 

 

    return {"role_overlay": overlay, "run_id": run_id} 

 

 

@traced(name="role_validator") 

def validator_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    role = state.get("user_role", "policy_user") 

    prompt = state.get("final_prompt", "") 

    overlay = state.get("role_overlay", {}) 

 

    eval_result = eval_role_output( 

        role=role, 

        prompt=prompt, 

        actual_output=json.dumps(overlay, ensure_ascii=True), 

        expected_output=json.dumps(overlay, ensure_ascii=True), 

    ) 

    blocked = bool(eval_result.get("enabled") and not eval_result.get("passed")) 

 

    return { 

        "eval_result": eval_result, 

        "blocked": blocked, 

        "block_reason": "deepeval_failed" if blocked else None, 

    } 

 

 

@traced(name="role_critic") 

def critic_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    """ 

    Lightweight critic: 

    - if validator failed, replace overlay with safer fallback 

    - else keep the existing overlay 

    """ 

    if state.get("blocked"): 

        role = state.get("user_role", "policy_user") 

        compare_response = state.get("compare_response", {}) 

        safer = _fallback_overlay(role, compare_response) 

        return {"role_overlay": safer} 

    return {} 

 

 

@traced(name="role_formatter") 

def finalize_node(state: dict) -> dict: 

    role = state.get("user_role", "policy_user") 

    compare_response = state.get("compare_response", {}) or {} 

    overlay = state.get("role_overlay", {}) or {} 

 

    base = format_response_for_role(compare_response, role) 

 

    if role == "policy_user": 

        base["summary"] = overlay.get("summary", base.get("summary", "")) 

        base["key_changes"] = overlay.get("key_changes", base.get("key_changes", [])) 

 

    elif role == "compliance_expert": 

        summary = base.get("summary", {}) 

        if not isinstance(summary, dict): 

            summary = {} 

        summary["overview"] = overlay.get("overview", summary.get("overview", "")) 

        summary["risk_notes"] = overlay.get("risk_notes", summary.get("risk_notes", [])) 

        summary["control_notes"] = overlay.get("control_notes", []) 

        base["summary"] = summary 

        base["risk_notes"] = overlay.get("risk_notes", base.get("risk_notes", [])) 

 

    elif role == "federal_officer": 

        base["summary"] = overlay.get("summary", base.get("summary", "")) 

        base["risk_notes"] = overlay.get("risk_notes", base.get("risk_notes", [])) 

        base["compliance_findings"] = overlay.get("compliance_findings", []) 

 

    else: 

        base["summary"] = overlay.get("summary", base.get("summary", "")) 

        base["audit_notes"] = overlay.get("audit_notes", []) 

        base["trace_comment"] = overlay.get("trace_comment", "") 

        base["metadata"] = { 

            "request_id": compare_response.get("request_id"), 

            "compare_id": compare_response.get("compare_id"), 

            "exact_cache_hit": compare_response.get("exact_cache_hit"), 

            "semantic_cache_hit": compare_response.get("semantic_cache_hit"), 

            "run_id": state.get("run_id"), 

            "deepeval": state.get("eval_result"), 

        } 

 

    # expose run_id on all roles too 

    base["run_id"] = state.get("run_id") 

    return {"final_response": base} 

from openai import OpenAI

from ..observability.langsmith_tracing import traced 

backend/app/agents/graph_enterprise.py 

from __future__ import annotations 

 

from langgraph.graph import StateGraph, END 

 

from .state_enterprise import EnterpriseAgentState 

from .roles import role_to_agent 

from .router import route_role_agent 

from .nodes_enterprise import ( 

    router_node, 

    specialist_prompt_node, 

    role_overlay_node, 

    validator_node, 

    critic_node, 

    finalize_node, 

) 

 

 

def build_enterprise_role_graph(): 

    g = StateGraph(EnterpriseAgentState) 

 

    g.add_node("router", router_node) 

    g.add_node("prompt_builder", specialist_prompt_node) 

    g.add_node("role_overlay", role_overlay_node) 

    g.add_node("validator", validator_node) 

    g.add_node("critic", critic_node) 

    g.add_node("finalize", finalize_node) 

 

    g.set_entry_point("router") 

 

    g.add_edge("router", "prompt_builder") 

    g.add_edge("prompt_builder", "role_overlay") 

    g.add_edge("role_overlay", "validator") 

    g.add_edge("validator", "critic") 

    g.add_edge("critic", "finalize") 

    g.add_edge("finalize", END) 

 

    return g.compile()

from __future__ import annotations 

 

import json 

from typing import Any 

 

from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, UploadFile, File, Request 

from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse 

 

from .auth_jwt import get_auth_context, AuthContext 

from .security.org_permissions import can_access_compare, CompareOwnerContext 

from .agents.graph_enterprise import build_enterprise_role_graph 

from .observability.langsmith_tracing import create_feedback 

from .utils import sse_event 

 

ENTERPRISE_ROLE_GRAPH = build_enterprise_role_graph() 

 

 

@app.post("/compare/role-aware/enterprise") 

async def compare_role_aware_enterprise( 

    request: Request, 

    auth: AuthContext = Depends(get_auth_context), 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

    file_a: UploadFile = File(...), 

    file_b: UploadFile = File(...), 

): 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

 

    # Reuse the existing compare core 

    base_resp = await _run_compare_core(request, file_a, file_b) 

    compare_dict = base_resp.model_dump() 

 

    owner_user_id = request.headers.get("X-Owner-User-Id") or auth["sub"] 

    owner_manager_id = request.headers.get("X-Owner-Manager-Id") or auth.get("manager_id") 

 

    allowed = can_access_compare( 

        requester_role=auth["role"], 

        requester_user_id=auth["sub"], 

        requester_manager_id=auth.get("manager_id"), 

        owner=CompareOwnerContext( 

            owner_user_id=owner_user_id, 

            owner_manager_id=owner_manager_id, 

        ), 

    ) 

    if not allowed: 

        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not allowed for this org scope") 

 

    evidence = [ 

        { 

            "source": "compare_summary", 

            "text": (compare_dict.get("summary") or {}).get("overview", ""), 

        }, 

        { 

            "source": "compare_diff", 

            "text": json.dumps(compare_dict.get("diff", {}), ensure_ascii=True), 

        }, 

    ] 

 

    state = { 

        "request_id": compare_dict.get("request_id"), 

        "user_id": auth["sub"], 

        "user_role": auth["role"], 

        "manager_id": auth.get("manager_id"), 

        "skip_manager_id": auth.get("skip_manager_id"), 

        "access_scope": ( 

            "audit" 

            if auth["role"] == "audit" 

            else "org" 

            if auth["role"] == "federal_officer" 

            else "team" 

            if auth["role"] == "compliance_expert" 

            else "self" 

        ), 

        "query": "Role-aware document comparison", 

        "compare_response": compare_dict, 

        "evidence": evidence, 

    } 

 

    result = ENTERPRISE_ROLE_GRAPH.invoke(state) 

    return result["final_response"] 

 

 

@app.post("/compare/role-aware/enterprise/stream") 

async def compare_role_aware_enterprise_stream( 

    request: Request, 

    auth: AuthContext = Depends(get_auth_context), 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

    file_a: UploadFile = File(...), 

    file_b: UploadFile = File(...), 

): 

    """ 

    Async SSE streaming: 

    - emits pipeline milestones 

    - emits final role-aware response 

    """ 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

    rid = request.state.request_id 

 

    async def gen(): 

        yield sse_event("start", json.dumps({"request_id": rid}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

 

        try: 

            base_resp = await _run_compare_core(request, file_a, file_b) 

            compare_dict = base_resp.model_dump() 

            yield sse_event("compare_complete", json.dumps({"compare_id": compare_dict.get("compare_id")}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

 

            owner_user_id = request.headers.get("X-Owner-User-Id") or auth["sub"] 

            owner_manager_id = request.headers.get("X-Owner-Manager-Id") or auth.get("manager_id") 

 

            allowed = can_access_compare( 

                requester_role=auth["role"], 

                requester_user_id=auth["sub"], 

                requester_manager_id=auth.get("manager_id"), 

                owner=CompareOwnerContext( 

                    owner_user_id=owner_user_id, 

                    owner_manager_id=owner_manager_id, 

                ), 

            ) 

            if not allowed: 

                err = {"request_id": rid, "detail": "Not allowed for this org scope"} 

                yield sse_event("error", json.dumps(err, separators=(",", ":"))) 

                return 

 

            evidence = [ 

                { 

                    "source": "compare_summary", 

                    "text": (compare_dict.get("summary") or {}).get("overview", ""), 

                }, 

                { 

                    "source": "compare_diff", 

                    "text": json.dumps(compare_dict.get("diff", {}), ensure_ascii=True), 

                }, 

            ] 

 

            state = { 

                "request_id": compare_dict.get("request_id"), 

                "user_id": auth["sub"], 

                "user_role": auth["role"], 

                "manager_id": auth.get("manager_id"), 

                "skip_manager_id": auth.get("skip_manager_id"), 

                "access_scope": ( 

                    "audit" 

                    if auth["role"] == "audit" 

                    else "org" 

                    if auth["role"] == "federal_officer" 

                    else "team" 

                    if auth["role"] == "compliance_expert" 

                    else "self" 

                ), 

                "query": "Role-aware document comparison", 

                "compare_response": compare_dict, 

                "evidence": evidence, 

            } 

 

            yield sse_event("graph_start", json.dumps({"role": auth["role"]}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

 

            result = ENTERPRISE_ROLE_GRAPH.invoke(state) 

            payload = result["final_response"] 

 

            yield sse_event("result", json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False)) 

            yield sse_event("done", json.dumps({"ok": True}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

        except Exception: 

            yield sse_event("error", json.dumps({"request_id": rid, "detail": "Internal error"}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

 

    return StreamingResponse(gen(), media_type="text/event-stream", headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache"}) 

 

 

@app.post("/feedback/enterprise") 

async def feedback_enterprise( 

    request: Request, 

    body: FeedbackRequest, 

    auth: AuthContext = Depends(get_auth_context), 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

): 

    """ 

    Forwards feedback to LangSmith when run_id is provided in metadata. 

    """ 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

 

    rid = request.state.request_id 

    cfg = settings.pii_config() 

    salt = settings.PII_HASH_SALT 

 

    body.comment = mask_text(body.comment or "", cfg, salt) 

 

    safe_meta: dict[str, Any] = {} 

    for k, v in (body.metadata or {}).items(): 

        safe_meta[str(k)] = mask_text(v, cfg, salt) if isinstance(v, str) else v 

    body.metadata = safe_meta 

 

    request.app.state.stores.put_feedback(rid, body) 

 

    run_id = None 

    if isinstance(body.metadata, dict): 

        run_id = body.metadata.get("run_id") 

 

    if isinstance(run_id, str) and run_id.strip(): 

        create_feedback( 

            run_id=run_id.strip(), 

            rating=body.rating, 

            comment=body.comment, 

            correction=(body.metadata.get("correction") if isinstance(body.metadata, dict) else None), 

        ) 

 

    return FeedbackResponse(request_id=rid, ok=True)

from __future__ import annotations

from . import __version__ 

from .agents import build_role_graph 

from .agents.graph_enterprise import build_enterprise_role_graph

from .auth_jwt import AuthContext, get_auth_context 

from .config import Settings 

from .diff import diff_docx, diff_pdf, diff_tabular, diff_text 

from .hashing import stable_compact_hash

app.state.exact_cache = ExactCache(ttl_seconds=settings.CACHE_TTL_SECONDS, max_items=settings.MAX_CACHE_ITEMS)

@app.middleware("http") 

async def audit_and_request_id(request: Request, call_next): 

    rid = get_request_id(request) 

    request.state.request_id = rid 

 

    start = time.time() 

    status = 500 

    api_key_present = bool(request.headers.get("X-API-Key") or request.headers.get("Authorization")) 

 

    try: 

        response = await call_next(request) 

        status = response.status_code 

        response.headers["X-Request-Id"] = rid 

        return response 

    finally: 

        latency_ms = int((time.time() - start) * 1000) 

        compare_id = getattr(request.state, "compare_id", None) 

        file_a_sha = getattr(request.state, "file_a_sha256", None) 

        file_b_sha = getattr(request.state, "file_b_sha256", None) 

        exact_hit = getattr(request.state, "exact_cache_hit", None) 

        semantic_hit = getattr(request.state, "semantic_cache_hit", None) 

        notes = "rate_limited" if getattr(request.state, "rate_limited", False) else None 

 

        app.state.audit.write( 

            AuditEvent( 

                ts=now_ts(), 

                request_id=rid, 

                path=str(request.url.path), 

                method=request.method, 

                status=status, 

                latency_ms=latency_ms, 

                api_key_present=api_key_present, 

                file_a_sha256=file_a_sha, 

                file_b_sha256=file_b_sha, 

                compare_id=compare_id, 

                exact_cache_hit=exact_hit, 

                semantic_cache_hit=semantic_hit, 

                notes=notes, 

            ) 

        ) 

 

 

@app.exception_handler(HTTPException) 

async def http_exc_handler(request: Request, exc: HTTPException): 

    rid = getattr(request.state, "request_id", str(uuid.uuid4())) 

    payload = ErrorResponse(request_id=rid, error="http_error", detail=str(exc.detail)) 

    return JSONResponse(status_code=exc.status_code, content=payload.model_dump()) 

 

 

@app.get("/health", response_model=HealthResponse) 

async def health(): 

    return HealthResponse(ok=True, service=settings.APP_NAME, version=__version__) 

 

 

def _file_meta(d) -> FileMeta: 

    return FileMeta( 

        filename=d.filename, 

        content_type=d.content_type, 

        size_bytes=d.size_bytes, 

        sha256=d.sha256, 

        detected_type=d.doc_type, 

    ) 

 

 

def _build_llm_prompt(diff_payload: DiffPayload, file_a: FileMeta, file_b: FileMeta, max_chars: int) -> str: 

    base = { 

        "mode": diff_payload.mode, 

        "file_a": {"name": file_a.filename, "type": file_a.detected_type, "sha256": file_a.sha256}, 

        "file_b": {"name": file_b.filename, "type": file_b.detected_type, "sha256": file_b.sha256}, 

    } 

 

    diff_part: dict[str, Any] = {"summary": {}} 

 

    if diff_payload.mode == "text" and diff_payload.text: 

        d = diff_payload.text 

        diff_part["summary"] = {"added": d.added, "removed": d.removed, "modified": d.modified, "truncated": d.truncated} 

        diff_part["items"] = [i.model_dump() for i in d.items[:80]] 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "docx" and diff_payload.docx: 

        d = diff_payload.docx 

        diff_part["summary"] = {"added": d.added, "removed": d.removed, "modified": d.modified, "truncated": d.truncated} 

        diff_part["items"] = [i.model_dump() for i in d.items[:80]] 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "tabular" and diff_payload.tabular: 

        d = diff_payload.tabular 

        diff_part["schema"] = d.schema.model_dump() 

        diff_part["cell_changes"] = [c.model_dump() for c in d.cell_changes[:120]] 

        diff_part["truncated"] = d.truncated 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "pdf" and diff_payload.pdf: 

        d = diff_payload.pdf 

        diff_part["pages"] = [ 

            {"page": p.page, "diff": {"added": p.diff.added, "removed": p.diff.removed, "modified": p.diff.modified}} 

            for p in d.pages[:12] 

        ] 

        snippets: list[dict[str, Any]] = [] 

        for p in d.pages[:6]: 

            snippets.extend([i.model_dump() for i in p.diff.items[:10]]) 

        diff_part["items"] = snippets 

        diff_part["truncated"] = d.truncated 

 

    prompt_obj = { 

        "task": "Summarize differences between two documents. Do not guess missing info. Keep concise and business-friendly.", 

        "requirements": { 

            "output_fields": ["status", "overview", "key_changes", "risk_notes", "confidence"], 

            "status_rule": "Use insufficient_evidence if diff content is empty or too truncated to be confident.", 

        }, 

        "context": base, 

        "diff": diff_part, 

    } 

 

    s = json.dumps(prompt_obj, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=True) 

    if len(s) > max_chars: 

        s = s[: max_chars - 20] + '"...TRUNCATED"}' 

    return ( 

        "You are a deterministic compliance-grade summarizer.\n" 

        "Return ONLY JSON matching the schema fields:\n" 

        '{"status":"ok|insufficient_evidence","overview":"...","key_changes":["..."],"risk_notes":["..."],"confidence":0.0}\n' 

        "Input:\n" 

        f"{s}" 

    ) 

 

 

def _make_llm(settings_obj: Settings) -> LLMClient: 

    if not settings_obj.OPENAI_API_KEY: 

        raise RuntimeError("OPENAI_API_KEY not set") 

    return LLMClient( 

        api_key=settings_obj.OPENAI_API_KEY, 

        model=settings_obj.OPENAI_MODEL, 

        embed_model=settings_obj.OPENAI_EMBEDDING_MODEL, 

        max_tokens=settings_obj.OPENAI_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS, 

        timeout_s=settings_obj.OUTBOUND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS, 

    ) 

 

 

def _compute_diff(settings_obj: Settings, a_doc, b_doc) -> DiffPayload: 

    max_items = settings_obj.MAX_DIFF_ITEMS 

 

    if a_doc.doc_type == "csv" and b_doc.doc_type == "csv": 

        td: TabularDiff = diff_tabular(a_doc.parsed, b_doc.parsed, max_items=max_items) 

        return DiffPayload(mode="tabular", tabular=td) 

 

    if a_doc.doc_type == "xlsx" and b_doc.doc_type == "xlsx": 

        td2: TabularDiff = diff_tabular(a_doc.parsed, b_doc.parsed, max_items=max_items) 

        return DiffPayload(mode="tabular", tabular=td2) 

 

    if a_doc.doc_type == "docx" and b_doc.doc_type == "docx": 

        a_paras = [p.get("text") or "" for p in (a_doc.parsed.get("paragraphs") or [])] 

        b_paras = [p.get("text") or "" for p in (b_doc.parsed.get("paragraphs") or [])] 

        dd: DocxDiff = diff_docx(a_paras, b_paras, max_items=max_items) 

        return DiffPayload(mode="docx", docx=dd) 

 

    if a_doc.doc_type == "pdf" and b_doc.doc_type == "pdf": 

        pd: PdfDiff = diff_pdf(a_doc.parsed.get("pages") or [], b_doc.parsed.get("pages") or [], max_items=max_items) 

        return DiffPayload(mode="pdf", pdf=pd) 

 

    a_text = a_doc.parsed.get("text") or "" 

    b_text = b_doc.parsed.get("text") or "" 

    td3: TextDiff = diff_text(a_text, b_text, max_items=max_items) 

    return DiffPayload(mode="text", text=td3) 

 

 

def _offline_summary(diff_payload: DiffPayload) -> LlmSummary: 

    empty = False 

    if diff_payload.mode == "text": 

        empty = not diff_payload.text or (diff_payload.text.added + diff_payload.text.removed + diff_payload.text.modified == 0) 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "docx": 

        empty = not diff_payload.docx or (diff_payload.docx.added + diff_payload.docx.removed + diff_payload.docx.modified == 0) 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "tabular": 

        empty = not diff_payload.tabular or ( 

            len(diff_payload.tabular.cell_changes) == 0 

            and not diff_payload.tabular.schema.added_columns 

            and not diff_payload.tabular.schema.removed_columns 

        ) 

    elif diff_payload.mode == "pdf": 

        empty = not diff_payload.pdf or (len(diff_payload.pdf.pages) == 0) 

 

    status = "insufficient_evidence" if empty else "ok" 

    overview = "OpenAI not configured; returning deterministic summary from diff statistics." 

    if status == "ok": 

        overview = "Differences detected; OpenAI not configured so detailed narrative summary is unavailable." 

    return LlmSummary(status=status, overview=overview, key_changes=[], risk_notes=[], confidence=0.3, cache_hit=False) 

 

 

def _mask_parsed(settings_obj: Settings, parsed: dict) -> dict: 

    if not parsed: 

        return parsed 

 

    cfg = settings_obj.pii_config() 

    salt = settings_obj.PII_HASH_SALT 

 

    def m(s: str) -> str: 

        return mask_text(s or "", cfg, salt) 

 

    out = dict(parsed) 

 

    if "text" in out and isinstance(out["text"], str): 

        out["text"] = m(out["text"]) 

 

    if "pages" in out and isinstance(out["pages"], list): 

        out["pages"] = [{**p, "text": m(p.get("text", ""))} if isinstance(p, dict) else p for p in out["pages"]] 

 

    if "paragraphs" in out and isinstance(out["paragraphs"], list): 

        out["paragraphs"] = [ 

            {**p, "text": m(p.get("text", ""))} if isinstance(p, dict) else p for p in out["paragraphs"] 

        ] 

 

    if "slides" in out and isinstance(out["slides"], list): 

        out["slides"] = [{**s, "text": m(s.get("text", ""))} if isinstance(s, dict) else s for s in out["slides"]] 

 

    if "sheets" in out and isinstance(out["sheets"], dict): 

        new_sheets = {} 

        for name, sheet in out["sheets"].items(): 

            if not isinstance(sheet, dict): 

                continue 

            sh2 = dict(sheet) 

            if isinstance(sh2.get("header"), list): 

                sh2["header"] = [m(str(x)) for x in sh2["header"]] 

            if isinstance(sh2.get("rows"), list): 

                sh2["rows"] = [[m(str(x)) for x in r] if isinstance(r, list) else r for r in sh2["rows"]] 

            new_sheets[str(name)] = sh2 

        out["sheets"] = new_sheets 

 

    return out 

 

 

async def _rate_limit(request: Request, api_key: str): 

    try: 

        request.app.state.rate_limiter.check(request, api_key) 

    except HTTPException: 

        request.state.rate_limited = True 

        raise 

 

 

async def _run_compare_core(request: Request, file_a: UploadFile, file_b: UploadFile) -> CompareResponse: 

    rid = request.state.request_id 

 

    a_doc = await parse_upload(file_a) 

    b_doc = await parse_upload(file_b) 

 

    a_doc.parsed = _mask_parsed(settings, a_doc.parsed) 

    b_doc.parsed = _mask_parsed(settings, b_doc.parsed) 

 

    request.state.file_a_sha256 = a_doc.sha256 

    request.state.file_b_sha256 = b_doc.sha256 

 

    key_parts = { 

        "a": a_doc.sha256, 

        "b": b_doc.sha256, 

        "types": [a_doc.doc_type, b_doc.doc_type], 

        "max_items": settings.MAX_DIFF_ITEMS, 

        "pii_cfg": settings.pii_config(), 

        "model": settings.OPENAI_MODEL, 

        "embed_model": settings.OPENAI_EMBEDDING_MODEL, 

        "max_tokens": settings.OPENAI_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS, 

        "threshold": settings.SEMANTIC_CACHE_THRESHOLD, 

    } 

    exact_key = stable_compact_hash(key_parts) 

 

    cached = request.app.state.exact_cache.get(exact_key) 

    if cached: 

        resp: CompareResponse = cached 

        resp2 = resp.model_copy(deep=True) 

        resp2.request_id = rid 

        resp2.exact_cache_hit = True 

        request.state.exact_cache_hit = True 

        request.state.semantic_cache_hit = getattr(resp2, "semantic_cache_hit", False) 

        request.state.compare_id = resp2.compare_id 

        return resp2 

 

    diff_payload = _compute_diff(settings, a_doc, b_doc) 

    file_meta_a = _file_meta(a_doc) 

    file_meta_b = _file_meta(b_doc) 

    prompt = _build_llm_prompt(diff_payload, file_meta_a, file_meta_b, settings.MAX_TEXT_CHARS_FOR_LLM) 

 

    semantic_hit = False 

    embedding = None 

 

    try: 

        llm = _make_llm(settings) 

        try: 

            emb_key = embedding_cache_key(settings.OPENAI_EMBEDDING_MODEL, prompt) 

            embedding = request.app.state.embedding_cache.get(emb_key) 

            if embedding is None: 

                embedding = llm.embed(prompt) 

                request.app.state.embedding_cache.set(emb_key, embedding) 

 

            semantic_hit, semantic_val = request.app.state.semantic_cache.lookup(embedding) 

            if semantic_hit and semantic_val: 

                resp_prev: CompareResponse = semantic_val 

                resp_prev2 = resp_prev.model_copy(deep=True) 

                resp_prev2.request_id = rid 

                resp_prev2.semantic_cache_hit = True 

                resp_prev2.summary.cache_hit = True 

                request.state.semantic_cache_hit = True 

                request.state.compare_id = resp_prev2.compare_id 

                request.app.state.exact_cache.set(exact_key, resp_prev2.model_copy(deep=True)) 

                request.state.exact_cache_hit = False 

                return resp_prev2 

 

            llm_summary = llm.summarize(prompt) 

        finally: 

            llm.close() 

    except Exception: 

        llm_summary = _offline_summary(diff_payload) 

 

    compare_id = str(uuid.uuid4()) 

    request.state.compare_id = compare_id 

 

    resp = CompareResponse( 

        request_id=rid, 

        compare_id=compare_id, 

        file_a=file_meta_a, 

        file_b=file_meta_b, 

        diff=diff_payload, 

        summary=llm_summary, 

        exact_cache_hit=False, 

        semantic_cache_hit=bool(semantic_hit), 

    ) 

 

    request.app.state.stores.put_compare(compare_id, resp) 

    request.app.state.exact_cache.set(exact_key, resp) 

 

    if embedding is not None and not semantic_hit: 

        hint = stable_compact_hash({"a": a_doc.sha256, "b": b_doc.sha256, "mode": diff_payload.mode, "t": time.time()}) 

        request.app.state.semantic_cache.insert(embedding=embedding, value=resp, key_hint=hint) 

 

    request.state.exact_cache_hit = False 

    request.state.semantic_cache_hit = bool(semantic_hit) 

    return resp 

 

 

@app.post("/compare", response_model=CompareResponse) 

async def compare( 

    request: Request, 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

    file_a: UploadFile = File(...), 

    file_b: UploadFile = File(...), 

): 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

    return await _run_compare_core(request, file_a, file_b) 

 

 

@app.post("/compare/stream") 

async def compare_stream( 

    request: Request, 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

    file_a: UploadFile = File(...), 

    file_b: UploadFile = File(...), 

): 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

    rid = request.state.request_id 

 

    async def gen(): 

        yield sse_event("start", json.dumps({"request_id": rid}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

        try: 

            base_resp = await _run_compare_core(request, file_a, file_b) 

            yield sse_event("result", base_resp.model_dump_json()) 

            yield sse_event("done", json.dumps({"ok": True}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

        except HTTPException as he: 

            err = ErrorResponse(request_id=rid, error="http_error", detail=str(he.detail)) 

            yield sse_event("error", err.model_dump_json()) 

        except Exception: 

            err = ErrorResponse(request_id=rid, error="internal_error", detail="Internal error") 

            yield sse_event("error", err.model_dump_json()) 

 

    return StreamingResponse(gen(), media_type="text/event-stream", headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache"}) 

 

 

@app.post("/compare/role-aware") 

async def compare_role_aware( 

    request: Request, 

    ctx: RequestContext = Depends(get_request_context), 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

    file_a: UploadFile = File(...), 

    file_b: UploadFile = File(...), 

): 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

 

    base_resp = await _run_compare_core(request, file_a, file_b) 

    compare_dict = base_resp.model_dump() 

 

    owner_user_id = request.headers.get("X-Owner-User-Id") or ctx.user_id 

    owner_manager_id = request.headers.get("X-Owner-Manager-Id") or ctx.manager_id 

 

    allowed = can_access_compare( 

        requester_role=ctx.user_role, 

        requester_user_id=ctx.user_id, 

        requester_manager_id=ctx.manager_id, 

        owner=CompareOwnerContext(owner_user_id=owner_user_id, owner_manager_id=owner_manager_id), 

    ) 

    if not allowed: 

        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not allowed for this org scope") 

 

    evidence = [ 

        { 

            "source": "compare_summary", 

            "text": (compare_dict.get("summary") or {}).get("overview", ""), 

        }, 

        { 

            "source": "compare_diff", 

            "text": json.dumps(compare_dict.get("diff", {}), ensure_ascii=True), 

        }, 

    ] 

 

    state = { 

        "request_id": compare_dict.get("request_id"), 

        "user_id": ctx.user_id, 

        "user_role": ctx.user_role, 

        "manager_id": ctx.manager_id, 

        "skip_manager_id": ctx.skip_manager_id, 

        "access_scope": ( 

            "audit" 

            if ctx.user_role == "audit" 

            else "org" 

            if ctx.user_role == "federal_officer" 

            else "team" 

            if ctx.user_role == "compliance_expert" 

            else "self" 

        ), 

        "query": "Role-aware document comparison", 

        "compare_response": compare_dict, 

        "evidence": evidence, 

    } 

 

    result = ROLE_GRAPH.invoke(state) 

    return result["final_response"]

@app.post("/compare/role-aware/enterprise/stream") 

async def compare_role_aware_enterprise_stream( 

    request: Request, 

    auth: AuthContext = Depends(get_auth_context), 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

    file_a: UploadFile = File(...), 

    file_b: UploadFile = File(...), 

): 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

    rid = request.state.request_id 

 

    async def gen(): 

        yield sse_event("start", json.dumps({"request_id": rid}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

        try: 

            base_resp = await _run_compare_core(request, file_a, file_b) 

            compare_dict = base_resp.model_dump() 

            yield sse_event("compare_complete", json.dumps({"compare_id": compare_dict.get("compare_id")}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

 

            owner_user_id = request.headers.get("X-Owner-User-Id") or auth["sub"] 

            owner_manager_id = request.headers.get("X-Owner-Manager-Id") or auth.get("manager_id") 

 

            allowed = can_access_compare( 

                requester_role=auth["role"], 

                requester_user_id=auth["sub"], 

                requester_manager_id=auth.get("manager_id"), 

                owner=CompareOwnerContext(owner_user_id=owner_user_id, owner_manager_id=owner_manager_id), 

            ) 

            if not allowed: 

                yield sse_event("error", json.dumps({"request_id": rid, "detail": "Not allowed for this org scope"}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

                return 

 

            evidence = [ 

                { 

                    "source": "compare_summary", 

                    "text": (compare_dict.get("summary") or {}).get("overview", ""), 

                }, 

                { 

                    "source": "compare_diff", 

                    "text": json.dumps(compare_dict.get("diff", {}), ensure_ascii=True), 

                }, 

            ] 

 

            state = { 

                "request_id": compare_dict.get("request_id"), 

                "user_id": auth["sub"], 

                "user_role": auth["role"], 

                "manager_id": auth.get("manager_id"), 

                "skip_manager_id": auth.get("skip_manager_id"), 

                "access_scope": ( 

                    "audit" 

                    if auth["role"] == "audit" 

                    else "org" 

                    if auth["role"] == "federal_officer" 

                    else "team" 

                    if auth["role"] == "compliance_expert" 

                    else "self" 

                ), 

                "query": "Role-aware document comparison", 

                "compare_response": compare_dict, 

                "evidence": evidence, 

            } 

 

            yield sse_event("graph_start", json.dumps({"role": auth["role"]}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

            result = ENTERPRISE_ROLE_GRAPH.invoke(state) 

            yield sse_event("result", json.dumps(result["final_response"], separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False)) 

            yield sse_event("done", json.dumps({"ok": True}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

        except HTTPException as he: 

            yield sse_event("error", json.dumps({"request_id": rid, "detail": str(he.detail)}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

        except Exception: 

            yield sse_event("error", json.dumps({"request_id": rid, "detail": "Internal error"}, separators=(",", ":"))) 

 

    return StreamingResponse(gen(), media_type="text/event-stream", headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache"}) 

 

 

@app.get("/export/summary", response_model=ExportSummaryResponse) 

async def export_summary( 

    request: Request, 

    compare_id: str, 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

): 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

    rid = request.state.request_id 

 

    rec = request.app.state.stores.get_compare(compare_id) 

    if not rec: 

        raise HTTPException(status_code=HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="compare_id not found or expired") 

 

    cfg = settings.pii_config() 

    salt = settings.PII_HASH_SALT 

    s = rec.summary.model_copy(deep=True) 

    s.overview = mask_text(s.overview, cfg, salt) 

    s.key_changes = [mask_text(x, cfg, salt) for x in s.key_changes] 

    s.risk_notes = [mask_text(x, cfg, salt) for x in s.risk_notes] 

 

    return ExportSummaryResponse( 

        request_id=rid, 

        compare_id=compare_id, 

        file_a=rec.file_a, 

        file_b=rec.file_b, 

        summary=s, 

    ) 

 

 

@app.post("/feedback", response_model=FeedbackResponse) 

async def feedback( 

    request: Request, 

    body: FeedbackRequest, 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

): 

    await _rate_limit(request, api_key) 

    rid = request.state.request_id 

 

    cfg = settings.pii_config() 

    salt = settings.PII_HASH_SALT 

    body.comment = mask_text(body.comment or "", cfg, salt) 

 

    safe_meta: dict[str, Any] = {} 

    for k, v in (body.metadata or {}).items(): 

        safe_meta[str(k)] = mask_text(v, cfg, salt) if isinstance(v, str) else v 

    body.metadata = safe_meta 

 

    request.app.state.stores.put_feedback(rid, body) 

    return FeedbackResponse(request_id=rid, ok=True) 

 

 

@app.post("/feedback/enterprise", response_model=FeedbackResponse) 

async def feedback_enterprise( 

    request: Request, 

    body: FeedbackRequest, 

    auth: AuthContext = Depends(get_auth_context), 

    api_key: str = Depends(require_api_key), 

):
